package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.entity;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.PhysicsMetadata;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.SpellContext;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.SpellExecutor;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.VisualMetadata;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.register.EntityRegister;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.ListTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.StringTag;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.Packet;
import net.minecraft.network.syncher.EntityDataAccessor;
import net.minecraft.network.syncher.EntityDataSerializers;
import net.minecraft.network.syncher.SynchedEntityData;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkHooks;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class EntityMagicSequence extends Entity {

    private static final EntityDataAccessor<Integer> CASTER_ID = SynchedEntityData.defineId(EntityMagicSequence.class, EntityDataSerializers.INT);
    private static final EntityDataAccessor<CompoundTag> PHYSICS_DATA = SynchedEntityData.defineId(EntityMagicSequence.class, EntityDataSerializers.COMPOUND_TAG);
    private static final EntityDataAccessor<CompoundTag> VISUAL_DATA = SynchedEntityData.defineId(EntityMagicSequence.class, EntityDataSerializers.COMPOUND_TAG);

    // 隧蜚ｱ譎る俣 (tick)
    private int castTime = 0;

    private int tickCounter = 0;
    private List<String> script = new ArrayList<>();
    private PhysicsMetadata physics = new PhysicsMetadata();
    private VisualMetadata visuals = new VisualMetadata();

    public EntityMagicSequence(EntityType<?> type, Level level) {
        super(type, level);
    }

    public EntityMagicSequence(Level level, LivingEntity caster, List<String> script, PhysicsMetadata physics, VisualMetadata visuals, int castTime) {
        this(EntityRegister.MAGIC_SEQUENCE.get(), level);
        if (caster != null) {
            this.entityData.set(CASTER_ID, caster.getId());
            this.setPos(caster.getX(), caster.getY(), caster.getZ());
        }
        this.script = script;
        this.physics = physics;
        this.visuals = visuals;
        this.castTime = castTime;

        this.entityData.set(PHYSICS_DATA, physics.toNBT());
        this.entityData.set(VISUAL_DATA, visuals.toNBT());
    }

    @Override
    protected void defineSynchedData() {
        this.entityData.define(CASTER_ID, -1);
        this.entityData.define(PHYSICS_DATA, new CompoundTag());
        this.entityData.define(VISUAL_DATA, new CompoundTag());
    }

    // 笆 霑ｽ蜉: 繝ｬ繝ｳ繝繝ｩ繝ｼ縺九ｉ繧｢繧ｯ繧ｻ繧ｹ縺吶ｋ縺溘ａ縺ｮGetter
    public int getCastTime() {
        return this.castTime;
    }

    // 繧ｯ繝ｩ繧､繧｢繝ｳ繝亥・縺ｧNBT蜷梧悄繝・・繧ｿ縺九ｉVisualMetadata繧貞ｾｩ蜈・☆繧九Γ繧ｽ繝・ラ
    public VisualMetadata getVisualMetadata() {
        CompoundTag tag = this.entityData.get(VISUAL_DATA);
        if (tag.isEmpty()) return new VisualMetadata();
        return VisualMetadata.fromNBT(tag);
    }

    @Override
    public void tick() {
        super.tick();
        if (!this.level.isClientSide) {
            tickCounter++;
            if (tickCounter >= castTime) {
                executeSpell();
                this.discard();
            }
        }
    }

    private void executeSpell() {
        if (!(this.level instanceof ServerLevel)) return;

        Entity casterEntity = this.level.getEntity(this.entityData.get(CASTER_ID));
        LivingEntity caster = (casterEntity instanceof LivingEntity) ? (LivingEntity) casterEntity : null;

        SpellContext ctx = new SpellContext();
        ctx.level = this.level;
        ctx.caster = caster;
        ctx.origin = this.position();
        ctx.script = this.script;

        ctx.physics = PhysicsMetadata.fromNBT(this.entityData.get(PHYSICS_DATA));
        ctx.visuals = VisualMetadata.fromNBT(this.entityData.get(VISUAL_DATA));

        ctx.fromSequence = true;
        SpellExecutor.execute(ctx);
    }

    @Override
    protected void readAdditionalSaveData(CompoundTag tag) {
        this.tickCounter = tag.getInt("Tick");
        this.castTime = tag.getInt("CastTime"); // 菫晏ｭ倥ョ繝ｼ繧ｿ縺ｮ隱ｭ縺ｿ霎ｼ縺ｿ
        this.entityData.set(CASTER_ID, tag.getInt("CasterID"));

        if (tag.contains("Script")) {
            ListTag list = tag.getList("Script", 8);
            script.clear();
            for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) {
                script.add(list.getString(i));
            }
        }
        if (tag.contains("Physics")) {
            this.entityData.set(PHYSICS_DATA, tag.getCompound("Physics"));
        }
        if (tag.contains("Visuals")) {
            this.entityData.set(VISUAL_DATA, tag.getCompound("Visuals"));
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void addAdditionalSaveData(CompoundTag tag) {
        tag.putInt("Tick", tickCounter);
        tag.putInt("CastTime", castTime); // 菫晏ｭ・
        tag.putInt("CasterID", this.entityData.get(CASTER_ID));

        ListTag list = new ListTag();
        for (String s : script) {
            list.add(StringTag.valueOf(s));
        }
        tag.put("Script", list);

        tag.put("Physics", this.entityData.get(PHYSICS_DATA));
        tag.put("Visuals", this.entityData.get(VISUAL_DATA));
    }

    @Override
    public Packet<?> getAddEntityPacket() {
        return NetworkHooks.getEntitySpawningPacket(this);
    }
}

FILE_PATH: C:\mincraft-mod\CollaboMod\src\main\java\com\COLLABOMOD\collabomod\entity\EntityMistDispersion.java

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.entity;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.register.EntityRegister;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.BlockParticleOption;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ItemParticleOption;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.Packet;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.ThrowableProjectile;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.BedBlock;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.DoorBlock;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.DoublePlantBlock;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BedPart;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.DoubleBlockHalf;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.BlockHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.EntityHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkHooks;

public class EntityMistDispersion extends ThrowableProjectile{
    public EntityMistDispersion(EntityType<? extends ThrowableProjectile> type, Level level) {
        super(type, level);
    }

    public EntityMistDispersion(Level level, LivingEntity shooter) {
        super(EntityRegister.MIST_DISPERSION.get(), shooter, level);
    }

    @Override
    protected void defineSynchedData() {}

    @Override
    public void tick() {
        super.tick();

        if (this.tickCount > 40) this.discard(); // 蟆・ｨ九・遏ｭ繧√〒繧０K・亥ｿ・ｮｺ謚縺ｪ縺ｮ縺ｧ・・
    }

    // 驥榊鴨縺ｪ縺暦ｼ育峩邱夊ｻ碁％・・
    @Override
    protected float getGravity() { return 0.0F; }

    @Override
    protected void onHit(HitResult result) {
        super.onHit(result);
        if (!this.level.isClientSide) {
            if (result.getType() != HitResult.Type.ENTITY) {
                this.discard();
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void onHitBlock(BlockHitResult result) {
        super.onHitBlock(result);
        if (!this.level.isClientSide) {
            BlockPos pos = result.getBlockPos();
            decomposeBlockRecursively(pos);
        }
    }

    // 騾｣邨舌ヶ繝ｭ繝・け繧ゅ∪縺ｨ繧√※豸医☆繝｡繧ｽ繝・ラ
    private void decomposeBlockRecursively(BlockPos pos) {
        BlockState state = this.level.getBlockState(pos);
        if (state.isAir()) return;

        // 蟯ｩ逶､縺ｪ縺ｩ縺ｯ髯､螟・
        if (state.getDestroySpeed(this.level, pos) < 0) return;

        // 笆 1. 騾｣邨舌ヱ繝ｼ繝・・讀懃ｴ｢縺ｨ蜑企勁
        // 繝峨い (DoorBlock)
        if (state.getBlock() instanceof DoorBlock) {
            DoubleBlockHalf half = state.getValue(DoorBlock.HALF);
            BlockPos otherPos = (half == DoubleBlockHalf.LOWER) ? pos.above() : pos.below();
            if (this.level.getBlockState(otherPos).getBlock() == state.getBlock()) {
                destroyBlockNoDrop(otherPos); // 逶ｸ譁ｹ繧貞・縺ｫ豸医☆
            }
        }
        // 繝吶ャ繝・(BedBlock)
        else if (state.getBlock() instanceof BedBlock) {
            BedPart part = state.getValue(BedBlock.PART);
            Direction facing = state.getValue(BedBlock.FACING);
            BlockPos otherPos = (part == BedPart.FOOT) ? pos.relative(facing) : pos.relative(facing.getOpposite());
            if (this.level.getBlockState(otherPos).getBlock() == state.getBlock()) {
                destroyBlockNoDrop(otherPos);
            }
        }
        // 閭後・鬮倥＞闕峨・闃ｱ (DoublePlantBlock)
        else if (state.getBlock() instanceof DoublePlantBlock) {
            DoubleBlockHalf half = state.getValue(DoublePlantBlock.HALF);
            BlockPos otherPos = (half == DoubleBlockHalf.LOWER) ? pos.above() : pos.below();
            if (this.level.getBlockState(otherPos).getBlock() == state.getBlock()) {
                destroyBlockNoDrop(otherPos);
            }
        }

        // 笆 2. 譛ｬ菴薙・蜑企勁縺ｨ貍泌・
        destroyBlockNoDrop(pos);
    }

    // 繝峨Ο繝・・縺ｪ縺励〒豸亥悉・・お繝輔ぉ繧ｯ繝育匱逕・
    private void destroyBlockNoDrop(BlockPos pos) {
        // 遨ｺ豌礼ｽｮ謠幢ｼ医ヵ繝ｩ繧ｰ2: 髫｣謗･譖ｴ譁ｰ縺ｪ縺励√ヵ繝ｩ繧ｰ16: 繝峨Ο繝・・縺ｪ縺暦ｼ・
        // 蠢ｵ縺ｮ縺溘ａ removeBlock(false) 繧剃ｽｿ縺・∪縺吶′縲√＆繧峨↓ setBlock 縺ｧ遒ｺ螳溘↓豸医＠縺ｾ縺・
        this.level.removeBlock(pos, false);

        // 貍泌・・夂ｲ峨↓縺ｪ縺｣縺ｦ蟠ｩ繧瑚誠縺｡繧・
        spawnDenseDustEffect(pos.getX() + 0.5, pos.getY() + 0.5, pos.getZ() + 0.5, 1.0);

        // 髻ｳ
        this.level.playSound(null, pos, SoundEvents.SAND_BREAK, SoundSource.BLOCKS, 1.0F, 2.0F);
    }

    // 笘・％縺薙′縲・騾｣蛻・ｧ｣縲阪・蜀咲樟繝ｭ繧ｸ繝・け
    @Override
    protected void onHitEntity(EntityHitResult result) {
        // super.onHitEntity縺ｯ蜻ｼ縺ｰ縺ｪ縺・ｼ郁ｲｫ騾壹＆縺帙↑縺・◆繧・ｼ・
        Entity target = result.getEntity();

        if (target instanceof LivingEntity livingTarget) {
            // 鬆伜沺蟷ｲ貂峨・諠・ｱ蠑ｷ蛹悶・蛻・ｧ｣
            livingTarget.removeAllEffects();
            if (livingTarget instanceof Player targetPlayer) {
                if (targetPlayer.isUsingItem() && targetPlayer.getUseItem().is(Items.SHIELD)) {
                    targetPlayer.getCooldowns().addCooldown(Items.SHIELD, 100);
                    targetPlayer.stopUsingItem();
                }
            }

            // 閧我ｽ灘・隗｣・亥叉豁ｻ・・
            livingTarget.hurt(DamageSource.OUT_OF_WORLD, Float.MAX_VALUE);

            // 貍泌・
            spawnDenseDustEffect(livingTarget.getX(), livingTarget.getY() + 1.0, livingTarget.getZ(), livingTarget.getBbWidth());

            // 髻ｳ・壹す繝･繧･繧･...縺ｨ縺・≧豸域ｻ・浹
            this.level.playSound(null, livingTarget.getX(), livingTarget.getY(), livingTarget.getZ(),
                    SoundEvents.FIRE_EXTINGUISH, target.getSoundSource(), 1.0F, 1.0F);
        }

        // 蠖薙◆縺｣縺溘ｉ蠑ｾ縺ｯ豸医∴繧・
        if (!this.level.isClientSide) {
            this.discard();
        }
    }

    // 笆笆笆 貍泌・蠑ｷ蛹也沿・壼､ｧ驥上・邊牙｡ｵ繧ｨ繝輔ぉ繧ｯ繝・笆笆笆
    private void spawnDenseDustEffect(double x, double y, double z, double widthScale) {
        if (this.level instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel) {

            // 1. 逋ｽ縺・ｲ・(White Concrete Powder) - 繝｡繧､繝ｳ縺ｮ谿矩ｪｸ
            // 驥上ｒ 30 -> 100 縺ｫ蠅鈴㍼
            BlockParticleOption dustParticle = new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.BLOCK, Blocks.WHITE_CONCRETE_POWDER.defaultBlockState());
            serverLevel.sendParticles(dustParticle, x, y, z,
                    500, widthScale * 0.5, 0.4, widthScale * 0.5, 0.0); // 遽・峇繧貞ｰ代＠邵ｦ髟ｷ縺ｫ

            // 2. 邊蛾妛 (Snowball) - 邏ｰ縺九＞邊貞ｭ先─
            // 繧｢繧､繝・Β繝代・繝・ぅ繧ｯ繝ｫ繧剃ｽｿ縺・→縲檎輔￠謨｣繧九肴─縺倥′蜃ｺ縺ｾ縺・
            ItemParticleOption snowParticle = new ItemParticleOption(ParticleTypes.ITEM, new ItemStack(Items.SNOWBALL));
            serverLevel.sendParticles(snowParticle, x, y, z,
                    500, widthScale * 0.2, 0.3, widthScale * 0.4, 0.05);

            // 3. 髴ｧ (Cloud) - 蛻・ｧ｣縺輔ｌ縺滓ｰ嶺ｽ・
            // 縺昴・蝣ｴ縺ｫ蟆代＠貍ゅｏ縺帙ｋ
            serverLevel.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.CLOUD, x, y, z,
                    10, widthScale * 0.5, 0.5, widthScale * 0.5, 0.02);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public Packet<?> getAddEntityPacket() {
        return NetworkHooks.getEntitySpawningPacket(this);
    }
}

==================================================
FILE_PATH: C:\mincraft-mod\CollaboMod\src\main\java\com\COLLABOMOD\collabomod\entity\EntitySciencePhenomenon.java
==================================================
package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.entity;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.PhysicsMetadata;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.VisualMetadata;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.register.EntityRegister; // 繧ｨ繝ｳ繝・ぅ繝・ぅ逋ｻ骭ｲ繧ｯ繝ｩ繧ｹ縺ｸ縺ｮ蜿ら・(迺ｰ蠅・↓蜷医ｏ縺帙※菫ｮ豁｣縺励※縺上□縺輔＞)
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.Packet;
import net.minecraft.network.syncher.EntityDataAccessor;
import net.minecraft.network.syncher.EntityDataSerializers;
import net.minecraft.network.syncher.SynchedEntityData;
import net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.MoverType;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkHooks;

import java.util.List;

public class EntitySciencePhenomenon extends Entity {

    // NBT繧ｿ繧ｰ繧貞酔譛溘☆繧九◆繧√・繝・・繧ｿ繧｢繧ｯ繧ｻ繧ｵ
    private static final EntityDataAccessor<CompoundTag> VISUAL_DATA = SynchedEntityData.defineId(EntitySciencePhenomenon.class, EntityDataSerializers.COMPOUND_TAG);
    private static final EntityDataAccessor<CompoundTag> PHYSICS_DATA = SynchedEntityData.defineId(EntitySciencePhenomenon.class, EntityDataSerializers.COMPOUND_TAG);
    private static final EntityDataAccessor<Integer> CASTER_ID = SynchedEntityData.defineId(EntitySciencePhenomenon.class, EntityDataSerializers.INT);

    // 繝ｭ繝ｼ繧ｫ繝ｫ繧ｭ繝｣繝・す繝･ (豈弱ヵ繝ｬ繝ｼ繝NBT隗｣譫舌☆繧九・繧帝亟縺舌◆繧・
    private PhysicsMetadata physicsCache = new PhysicsMetadata();
    private VisualMetadata visualCache = new VisualMetadata();
    private boolean isVisualCacheDirty = true;
    private boolean isPhysicsCacheDirty = true;

    public int lifeTime = 0;
    public int maxLifeTime = 100;
    private boolean hasExploded = false; // RADIAL繧ｿ繧､繝励・荳蝗樊ｧ辷・匱蛻ｶ蠕｡逕ｨ

    // 繝・ヵ繧ｩ繝ｫ繝医さ繝ｳ繧ｹ繝医Λ繧ｯ繧ｿ (逋ｻ骭ｲ逕ｨ)
    public EntitySciencePhenomenon(EntityType<?> type, Level level) {
        super(type, level);
        this.noCulling = true; // 蟶ｸ縺ｫ謠冗判
    }

    // 繧ｹ繝昴・繝ｳ逕ｨ繧ｳ繝ｳ繧ｹ繝医Λ繧ｯ繧ｿ
    public EntitySciencePhenomenon(Level level, LivingEntity caster) {
        this(EntityRegister.SCIENCE_PHENOMENON.get(), level);
        if (caster != null) {
            this.setPos(caster.getX(), caster.getEyeY(), caster.getZ());
            this.entityData.set(CASTER_ID, caster.getId());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void defineSynchedData() {
        // 繝・ヵ繧ｩ繝ｫ繝医・遨ｺ繝・・繧ｿ繧偵そ繝・ヨ
        this.entityData.define(VISUAL_DATA, new CompoundTag());
        this.entityData.define(PHYSICS_DATA, new CompoundTag());
        this.entityData.define(CASTER_ID, -1);
    }

    /**
     * 迚ｩ逅・ヱ繝ｩ繝｡繝ｼ繧ｿ繧定ｨｭ螳壹＠縲∵嫌蜍輔→蟇ｿ蜻ｽ繧貞・譛溷喧縺吶ｋ
     */
    public void setPhysicsMetadata(PhysicsMetadata p) {
        this.physicsCache = p;
        this.entityData.set(PHYSICS_DATA, p.toNBT()); // 繧ｯ繝ｩ繧､繧｢繝ｳ繝亥酔譛・
        this.isPhysicsCacheDirty = false;

        // 謖吝虚繧ｿ繧､繝励↓蝓ｺ縺･縺丞ｯｿ蜻ｽ險ｭ螳・
        if (p.forceType == PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.DIRECTIONAL) {
            this.maxLifeTime = 100; // 蟆・・邉ｻ縺ｯ鬟幄ｷ晞屬蛻ｶ髯・
        } else if (p.forceType == PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.RADIAL) {
            this.maxLifeTime = 20;  // 辷・匱縺ｯ荳迸ｬ縺ｮ繧ｨ繝輔ぉ繧ｯ繝・
        } else if (p.forceType == PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.FIELD) {
            this.maxLifeTime = 400; // 險ｭ鄂ｮ蝙九・髟ｷ譎る俣邯ｭ謖・
        } else {
            this.maxLifeTime = 60; // 縺昴・莉・
        }
    }

    /**
     * 隕冶ｦ壹ヱ繝ｩ繝｡繝ｼ繧ｿ繧定ｨｭ螳壹☆繧・
     */
    public void setVisualMetadata(VisualMetadata v) {
        this.visualCache = v;
        this.entityData.set(VISUAL_DATA, v.toNBT()); // 繧ｯ繝ｩ繧､繧｢繝ｳ繝亥酔譛・
        this.isVisualCacheDirty = false;
    }

    public PhysicsMetadata getPhysicsMetadata() {
        if (this.level.isClientSide && isPhysicsCacheDirty) {
            this.physicsCache = PhysicsMetadata.fromNBT(this.entityData.get(PHYSICS_DATA));
            isPhysicsCacheDirty = false;
        }
        return this.physicsCache;
    }

    public VisualMetadata getVisualMetadata() {
        if (this.level.isClientSide && isVisualCacheDirty) {
            this.visualCache = VisualMetadata.fromNBT(this.entityData.get(VISUAL_DATA));
            isVisualCacheDirty = false;
        }
        return this.visualCache;
    }

    @Override
    public void onSyncedDataUpdated(EntityDataAccessor<?> key) {
        super.onSyncedDataUpdated(key);
        if (VISUAL_DATA.equals(key)) {
            this.isVisualCacheDirty = true;
        } else if (PHYSICS_DATA.equals(key)) {
            this.isPhysicsCacheDirty = true;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void tick() {
        super.tick();
        this.lifeTime++;

        if (!this.level.isClientSide) {
            if (this.lifeTime > this.maxLifeTime) {
                this.discard();
                return;
            }
        }

        PhysicsMetadata phy = getPhysicsMetadata();
        VisualMetadata vis = getVisualMetadata();

        // --- 1. 遘ｻ蜍輔Ο繧ｸ繝・け ---
        if (phy.velocity > 0 || phy.gravity) {
            Vec3 motion = this.getDeltaMovement();

            // 驥榊鴨驕ｩ逕ｨ
            if (phy.gravity) {
                motion = motion.add(0, -0.04, 0);
            }

            // 遨ｺ豌玲慣謚・(邁｡譏・
            motion = motion.scale(0.98);

            this.setDeltaMovement(motion);
            this.move(MoverType.SELF, motion);
        }

        // --- 2. 謗･隗ｦ繝ｻ蜉ｹ譫憺←逕ｨ繝ｭ繧ｸ繝・け (繧ｵ繝ｼ繝舌・縺ｮ縺ｿ) ---
        if (!this.level.isClientSide) {
            // 蠖薙◆繧雁愛螳壹・繧ｵ繧､繧ｺ縺ｯ隕九°縺代・繧ｹ繧ｱ繝ｼ繝ｫ縺ｫ萓晏ｭ倥＆縺帙ｋ
            float radius = vis.scale * 0.5f;
            if (phy.forceType == PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.RADIAL) {
                // 辷・匱邉ｻ縺ｯ譎る俣邨碁℃縺ｧ遽・峇縺悟ｺ・′繧区ｼ泌・縺ｫ蜷医ｏ縺帙ｋ
                float progress = Math.min(1.0f, (float)lifeTime / 10.0f);
                radius = vis.scale * progress * 2.0f; // 譛螟ｧ2蛟阪∪縺ｧ蠎・′繧・
            }

            AABB searchBox = this.getBoundingBox().inflate(radius);
            List<Entity> targets = this.level.getEntities(this, searchBox);

            int casterId = this.entityData.get(CASTER_ID);

            for (Entity target : targets) {
                // 閾ｪ蛻・・霄ｫ繧・｡楢・・髯､螟・(縺溘□縺玲凾髢薙′邨後※縺ｰ陦楢・↓繧ょｽ薙◆繧・
                if (target.getId() == casterId && lifeTime < 10) continue;
                if (target instanceof EntitySciencePhenomenon) continue;

                // 遞ｮ鬘槭＃縺ｨ縺ｮ蜃ｦ逅・
                if (phy.forceType == PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.RADIAL) {
                    // 辷・匱・壻ｸ蠎ｦ縺縺代ム繝｡繝ｼ繧ｸ繧剃ｸ弱∴縺ｦ豸域ｻ・√∪縺溘・陦晄茶繧剃ｸ弱∴繧・
                    if (!hasExploded) {
                        applyPhysicsEffect(target, phy);
                    }
                }
                else if (phy.forceType == PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.DIRECTIONAL) {
                    // 蟆・・・壼ｽ薙◆縺｣縺溘ｉ蜉ｹ譫懊ｒ荳弱∴縺ｦ豸域ｻ・
                    applyPhysicsEffect(target, phy);
                    this.discard(); // 繝偵ャ繝医＠縺溘ｉ豸医∴繧・
                    return;
                }
                else if (phy.forceType == PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.FIELD) {
                    // 險ｭ鄂ｮ・夂ｯ・峇蜀・↓縺・ｋ髢薙★縺｣縺ｨ蜉ｹ譫・(5tick縺ｫ1蝗槭↑縺ｩ髢灘ｼ輔￥)
                    if (lifeTime % 5 == 0) {
                        applyPhysicsEffect(target, phy);
                    }
                }
            }

            if (phy.forceType == PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.RADIAL && !targets.isEmpty()) {
                hasExploded = true; // 辷・匱蛻､螳壽ｸ医∩
            }
        }
    }

    // 迚ｩ逅・ヱ繝ｩ繝｡繝ｼ繧ｿ縺ｫ蝓ｺ縺･縺上ム繝｡繝ｼ繧ｸ繝ｻ蜉ｹ譫懆ｨ育ｮ・
    private void applyPhysicsEffect(Entity target, PhysicsMetadata phy) {
        // 1. 辭ｱ繧ｨ繝阪Ν繧ｮ繝ｼ (轣ｫ蛯ｷ)
        if (phy.temperature > 1000.0F) {
            target.setSecondsOnFire(5);
            target.hurt(DamageSource.IN_FIRE, phy.energy * 0.05F);
        } else if (phy.temperature < 200.0F) {
            target.setTicksFrozen(200); // 蜃咲ｵ・
            target.hurt(DamageSource.FREEZE, phy.energy * 0.05F);
        }

        // 2. 迚ｩ逅・｡晄茶 (驕句虚繧ｨ繝阪Ν繧ｮ繝ｼ)
        // F = m * a 縺縺後√％縺薙〒縺ｯ Mass * Velocity 繧貞ｨ∝鴨菫よ焚縺ｨ縺吶ｋ
        float impact = phy.mass * phy.velocity;
        if (impact > 1.0F) {
            target.hurt(DamageSource.GENERIC, impact);

            // 繝弱ャ繧ｯ繝舌ャ繧ｯ
            Vec3 knockbackDir = this.position().vectorTo(target.position()).normalize();
            if (phy.forceType == PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.DIRECTIONAL) {
                knockbackDir = this.getDeltaMovement().normalize();
            }
            target.push(knockbackDir.x * impact * 0.1, 0.2, knockbackDir.z * impact * 0.1);
        }

        // 3. 邏皮ｲ九お繝阪Ν繧ｮ繝ｼ (鬲疲ｳ輔ム繝｡繝ｼ繧ｸ)
        if (phy.energy > 0) {
            target.hurt(DamageSource.MAGIC, phy.energy * 0.1F);
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void readAdditionalSaveData(CompoundTag tag) {
        this.lifeTime = tag.getInt("LifeTime");
        this.maxLifeTime = tag.getInt("MaxLifeTime");
        this.hasExploded = tag.getBoolean("HasExploded");

        if (tag.contains("PhysicsData")) {
            PhysicsMetadata p = PhysicsMetadata.fromNBT(tag.getCompound("PhysicsData"));
            this.setPhysicsMetadata(p);
        }
        if (tag.contains("VisualData")) {
            VisualMetadata v = VisualMetadata.fromNBT(tag.getCompound("VisualData"));
            this.setVisualMetadata(v);
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void addAdditionalSaveData(CompoundTag tag) {
        tag.putInt("LifeTime", lifeTime);
        tag.putInt("MaxLifeTime", maxLifeTime);
        tag.putBoolean("HasExploded", hasExploded);

        tag.put("PhysicsData", getPhysicsMetadata().toNBT());
        tag.put("VisualData", getVisualMetadata().toNBT());
    }

    @Override
    public Packet<?> getAddEntityPacket() {
        return NetworkHooks.getEntitySpawningPacket(this);
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.event;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.main.CollaboMod;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.world.cardinal.WorldCardinalSystem;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraftforge.event.TickEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;

@Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid = CollaboMod.MOD_ID)
public class CardinalTickHandler {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onWorldTick(TickEvent.WorldTickEvent event) {
        // 繧ｵ繝ｼ繝舌・蛛ｴ縺ｮTick邨ゆｺ・凾縺ｮ縺ｿ螳溯｡・
        if (event.side.isServer() && event.phase == TickEvent.Phase.END) {
            if (event.world instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel) {
                // 繧ｫ繝ｼ繝・ぅ繝翫Ν繧ｷ繧ｹ繝・Β繧貞叙蠕励＠縺ｦ譎ゅｒ騾ｲ繧√ｋ
                WorldCardinalSystem cardinal = WorldCardinalSystem.get(serverLevel);
                cardinal.tick(serverLevel);
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.event;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.capability.MagicStatsProvider;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.main.CollaboMod;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.world.idea.IdeaDimensionData;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.living.LivingDamageEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;

@Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid = CollaboMod.MOD_ID)
public class IdeaEvents {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onLivingDamage(LivingDamageEvent event) {
        LivingEntity entity = event.getEntityLiving();

        if (entity.level.isClientSide) return;

        // 笆 1. 繧ｨ繧､繝峨せ縺ｮ險倬鹸・亥・謌千畑繝舌ャ繧ｯ繧｢繝・・・・
        if (entity.level instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel) {
            IdeaDimensionData idea = IdeaDimensionData.get(serverLevel);
            // 繝繝｡繝ｼ繧ｸ繧貞女縺代ｋ逶ｴ蜑阪・迥ｶ諷九ｒ菫晏ｭ・
            idea.recordEntityState(entity, serverLevel.getGameTime());
        }

        // 笆 2. 閾ｪ蟾ｱ菫ｮ蠕ｩ陦灘ｼ擾ｼ医・繝ｬ繧､繝､繝ｼ蟆ら畑繧ｪ繝ｼ繝医ヲ繝ｼ繝ｫ・・
        if (entity instanceof Player player) {
            player.getCapability(MagicStatsProvider.PLAYER_MAGIC_STATS).ifPresent(stats -> {

                // 繝繝｡繝ｼ繧ｸ驥・
                float damage = event.getAmount();

                // 螂郁誠繝繝｡繝ｼ繧ｸ縺ｪ縺ｩ縺ｯ菫ｮ蠕ｩ荳榊庄閭ｽ縺ｨ縺励※髯､螟厄ｼ亥叉豁ｻ蝗樣∩縺ｮ縺溘ａ・・
                if (event.getSource().isBypassInvul()) return;

                // 繧ｳ繧ｹ繝郁ｨ育ｮ暦ｼ壹ム繝｡繝ｼ繧ｸ1縺ｫ縺､縺阪し繧､繧ｪ繝ｳ10
                int cost = (int)(damage * 10);

                // 繧ｵ繧､繧ｪ繝ｳ縺瑚ｶｳ繧翫※縺・ｌ縺ｰ逋ｺ蜍・
                if (stats.getCurrentPsion() >= cost) {

                    // 繝繝｡繝ｼ繧ｸ辟｡蜉ｹ蛹厄ｼ茨ｼ昴お繧､繝峨せ螟画峩縺ｮ諡堤ｵｶ・・
                    event.setCanceled(true);

                    // 繧ｳ繧ｹ繝域ｶ郁ｲｻ
                    stats.setCurrentPsion(stats.getCurrentPsion() - cost);

                    // 笆 莉｣蜆滂ｼ夂ｲｾ逾櫁ｲ闕ｷ・郁協逞幢ｼ・
                    // 逞帙∩縺ｯ豸医○縺ｪ縺・・縺ｧ縲√ム繝｡繝ｼ繧ｸ縺ｫ蠢懊§縺ｦ繧ｹ繝医Ξ繧ｹ縺梧ｺ懊∪繧・
                    // 繝繝｡繝ｼ繧ｸ縺悟､ｧ縺阪＞縺ｻ縺ｩ蠑ｷ辜医↑雋闕ｷ
                    int pain = (int)(damage * 5);
                    stats.addMentalLoad(pain);

                    // 貍泌・・夊・蛻・↓縺縺題◇縺薙∴繧矩浹
                    // "繧ｭ繝ｳ繝・縺ｨ縺・≧蠑ｾ縺上ｈ縺・↑髻ｳ縺ｧ縺ｯ縺ｪ縺上・繧ｷ繝･繧ｦ..."縺ｨ縺・≧菫ｮ蠕ｩ髻ｳ
                    player.level.playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY(), player.getZ(),
                            SoundEvents.BEACON_AMBIENT, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.5F, 2.0F);

                    // 繝・ヰ繝・げ逕ｨ繝ｭ繧ｰ・亥ｾ後〒豸医＠縺ｦ縺上□縺輔＞・・
                    // player.sendMessage(new net.minecraft.network.chat.TextComponent("ﾂｧb閾ｪ蟾ｱ菫ｮ蠕ｩ: " + damage + "dmg 辟｡蜉ｹ蛹・(Stress+" + pain + ")"), net.minecraft.Util.NIL_UUID);
                }
            });
        }
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.gui;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.block.entity.MagicConsoleBlockEntity;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.item.ICAD;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.register.BlockRegister;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.register.MenuTypeRegister;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.*;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.minecraftforge.items.CapabilityItemHandler;
import net.minecraftforge.items.IItemHandler;
import net.minecraftforge.items.SlotItemHandler;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

public class MagicConsoleMenu extends AbstractContainerMenu {

    public final MagicConsoleBlockEntity blockEntity;
    private final ContainerLevelAccess access;

    // 笆 菫ｮ豁｣: 繧ｯ繝ｩ繧､繧｢繝ｳ繝亥・繧ｳ繝ｳ繧ｹ繝医Λ繧ｯ繧ｿ
    public MagicConsoleMenu(int id, Inventory inv, FriendlyByteBuf extraData) {
        super(MenuTypeRegister.MAGIC_CONSOLE_MENU.get(), id);

        BlockPos pos = extraData.readBlockPos();
        this.blockEntity = (MagicConsoleBlockEntity) inv.player.level.getBlockEntity(pos);
        this.access = ContainerLevelAccess.create(inv.player.level, pos);

        // 繧ｯ繝ｩ繧､繧｢繝ｳ繝亥・縺ｧ繧５ileEntity縺九ｉ豁｣縺励＞Handler繧貞叙蠕励☆繧・
        // 蜿門ｾ励〒縺阪↑縺・ｴ蜷医・縺ｿ繝繝溘・繧剃ｽｿ縺・ｼ磯壼ｸｸ縺ｯ蜷梧悄縺輔ｌ縺ｦ縺・ｋ縺ｯ縺夲ｼ・
        IItemHandler handler = null;
        if (this.blockEntity != null) {
            handler = this.blockEntity.getCapability(CapabilityItemHandler.ITEM_HANDLER_CAPABILITY).orElse(null);
        }
        // 荳・′荳蜿門ｾ怜､ｱ謨励＠縺溷ｴ蜷医・縲∵桃菴懊〒縺阪↑縺・ｈ縺・↓遨ｺ縺ｮ繝繝溘・縺ｧ縺ｯ縺ｪ縺熟ull謇ｱ縺・〒繧ｨ繝ｩ繝ｼ蝗樣∩縺励※繧ゅ＞縺・′縲・
        // 縺薙％縺ｧ縺ｯ螳牙・遲悶→縺励※TileEntity縺檎┌縺代ｌ縺ｰ繧ｹ繝ｭ繝・ヨ繧定ｿｽ蜉縺励↑縺・ｭ峨・蛻・ｲ舌ｂ蜿ｯ閭ｽ縲・
        // 荳譌ｦ蜿門ｾ励〒縺阪◆蜑肴署縺ｧ騾ｲ繧√ｋ縲・

        if (handler != null) {
            addConsoleSlots(handler);
        }

        layoutPlayerInventory(inv, 48, 174);
    }

    // 笆 菫ｮ豁｣: 繧ｵ繝ｼ繝舌・蛛ｴ繧ｳ繝ｳ繧ｹ繝医Λ繧ｯ繧ｿ
    public MagicConsoleMenu(int id, Inventory inv, IItemHandler handler, BlockEntity entity) {
        super(MenuTypeRegister.MAGIC_CONSOLE_MENU.get(), id);
        this.blockEntity = (MagicConsoleBlockEntity) entity;
        this.access = ContainerLevelAccess.create(inv.player.level, entity.getBlockPos());

        addConsoleSlots(handler);
        layoutPlayerInventory(inv, 48, 174);
    }

    // 蜈ｱ騾壹・繧ｹ繝ｭ繝・ヨ霑ｽ蜉繝ｭ繧ｸ繝・け
    private void addConsoleSlots(IItemHandler handler) {
        this.addSlot(new SlotItemHandler(handler, 0, 42, 115) {
            @Override
            public boolean mayPlace(@NotNull ItemStack stack) {
                // ICAD縺ｮ縺ｿ險ｱ蜿ｯ
                return stack.getItem() instanceof ICAD;
            }
        });
    }

    private void layoutPlayerInventory(Inventory playerInventory, int leftCol, int topRow) {
        // 繝｡繧､繝ｳ繧､繝ｳ繝吶Φ繝医Μ
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
            for (int l = 0; l < 9; ++l) {
                this.addSlot(new Slot(playerInventory, l + i * 9 + 9, leftCol + l * 18, topRow + i * 18));
            }
        }
        // 繝帙ャ繝医ヰ繝ｼ
        for (int i = 0; i < 9; ++i) {
            this.addSlot(new Slot(playerInventory, i, leftCol + i * 18, topRow + 58));
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean stillValid(Player player) {
        return stillValid(this.access, player, BlockRegister.MAGIC_CONSOLE.get());
    }

    @Override
    public ItemStack quickMoveStack(Player player, int index) {
        ItemStack itemstack = ItemStack.EMPTY;
        Slot slot = this.slots.get(index);

        if (slot != null && slot.hasItem()) {
            ItemStack itemstack1 = slot.getItem();
            itemstack = itemstack1.copy();

            if (index == 0) { // CAD -> Player
                if (!this.moveItemStackTo(itemstack1, 1, 37, true)) {
                    return ItemStack.EMPTY;
                }
            } else { // Player -> CAD
                // SlotItemHandler(index 0) 縺ｫ遘ｻ蜍輔ｒ隧ｦ縺ｿ繧・
                if (itemstack1.getItem() instanceof ICAD) {
                    if (!this.moveItemStackTo(itemstack1, 0, 1, false)) {
                        return ItemStack.EMPTY;
                    }
                } else if (index < 28) {
                    if (!this.moveItemStackTo(itemstack1, 28, 37, false)) {
                        return ItemStack.EMPTY;
                    }
                } else if (index < 37 && !this.moveItemStackTo(itemstack1, 1, 28, false)) {
                    return ItemStack.EMPTY;
                }
            }

            if (itemstack1.isEmpty()) {
                slot.set(ItemStack.EMPTY);
            } else {
                slot.setChanged();
            }
        }
        return itemstack;
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.item;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.capability.MagicStatsProvider;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.entity.EntityMagicSequence;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.*;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.main.CollaboMod;
import net.minecraft.nbt.ListTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.StringTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.Tag;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.util.PsionParticleUtil;
import net.minecraft.Util;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.TextComponent;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResultHolder;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.ProjectileUtil;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.TooltipFlag;
import net.minecraft.world.item.UseAnim;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.EntityHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

import javax.annotation.Nullable;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;

public class ItemCAD extends Item {

    private final Random random = new Random();

    public ItemCAD() {
        super(new Item.Properties().tab(CollaboMod.COLLABOMOD_TAB).stacksTo(1));
    }

    @Override
    public InteractionResultHolder<ItemStack> use(Level level, Player player, InteractionHand hand) {
        ItemStack stack = player.getItemInHand(hand);

        if (!level.isClientSide) {
            CastMagic(level, player, stack);
            // 鬲疲ｳ穂ｽｿ逕ｨ譎ゅ・繧ｯ繝ｼ繝ｫ繝繧ｦ繝ｳ縺ｪ縺ｩ繧定ｨｭ螳壹☆繧句ｴ蜷医・縺薙％縺ｫ險倩ｿｰ
            player.getCooldowns().addCooldown(this, 10);
        }

        return InteractionResultHolder.success(stack);
    }

    private void CastMagic(Level level, Player player, ItemStack stack) {
        CompoundTag tag = stack.getOrCreateTag();
        List<String> script = new ArrayList<>();

        if (tag.contains("Script")) {
            ListTag list = tag.getList("Script", Tag.TAG_STRING);
            for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) {
                script.add(list.getString(i));
            }
        } else {
            // 繝・ヵ繧ｩ繝ｫ繝医・繧ｹ繧ｯ繝ｪ繝励ヨ (繝・せ繝育畑)
            script.add("Action.Explode()");
            script.add("Attr.Fire()");
            script.add("Cast()");
        }

        // 笆 菫ｮ豁｣邂・園: SpellContext 縺ｮ逕滓・譁ｹ豕輔ｒ螟画峩
        // SpellContext ctx = new SpellContext(level, player); // 譌ｧ繧ｳ繝ｼ繝・(繧ｨ繝ｩ繝ｼ)

        SpellContext ctx = new SpellContext(); // 譁ｰ繧ｳ繝ｼ繝・(蠑墓焚縺ｪ縺励さ繝ｳ繧ｹ繝医Λ繧ｯ繧ｿ)
        ctx.level = level;
        ctx.caster = player;
        ctx.script = script;

        // 蜴溽せ縺ｮ險ｭ螳夲ｼ育岼縺ｮ菴咲ｽｮ・・
        ctx.origin = player.getEyePosition();

        // 繧ｿ繝ｼ繧ｲ繝・ヨ蜿門ｾ・(繧ｪ繝励す繝ｧ繝ｳ: 隕也ｷ壹・蜈医・繧ｨ繝ｳ繝・ぅ繝・ぅ繧・ヶ繝ｭ繝・け繧偵ち繝ｼ繧ｲ繝・ヨ縺ｫ縺吶ｋ蝣ｴ蜷・
        HitResult result = getRayTraceResult(level, player, 20.0);
        if (result.getType() == HitResult.Type.ENTITY) {
            EntityHitResult entityResult = (EntityHitResult) result;
            if (entityResult.getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity) {
                ctx.target = (LivingEntity) entityResult.getEntity();
            }
        }

        // 螳溯｡・
        SpellExecutor.execute(ctx);

        // 繝輔ぅ繝ｼ繝峨ヰ繝・け
        player.sendMessage(new TextComponent("Magic Executed!"), Util.NIL_UUID);
    }

    @Override
    public void appendHoverText(ItemStack stack, @Nullable Level level, List<net.minecraft.network.chat.Component> tooltip, TooltipFlag flag) {
        if (stack.hasTag() && stack.getTag().contains("Script")) {
            tooltip.add(new TextComponent("ﾂｧaScript Loaded"));
            ListTag list = stack.getTag().getList("Script", Tag.TAG_STRING);
            for(int i=0; i<Math.min(list.size(), 3); i++) {
                tooltip.add(new TextComponent("ﾂｧ7" + list.getString(i)));
            }
            if(list.size() > 3) tooltip.add(new TextComponent("ﾂｧ7..."));
        } else {
            tooltip.add(new TextComponent("ﾂｧcNo Script"));
        }
    }

    // 繝ｬ繧､繧ｭ繝｣繧ｹ繝育畑繝倥Ν繝代・
    private HitResult getRayTraceResult(Level level, Player player, double range) {
        Vec3 eyePos = player.getEyePosition();
        Vec3 lookVec = player.getLookAngle();
        Vec3 endPos = eyePos.add(lookVec.scale(range));

        // 縺ｾ縺壹お繝ｳ繝・ぅ繝・ぅ蛻､螳・
        AABB searchBox = player.getBoundingBox().expandTowards(lookVec.scale(range)).inflate(1.0D);
        EntityHitResult entityResult = ProjectileUtil.getEntityHitResult(
                level, player, eyePos, endPos, searchBox, (e) -> !e.isSpectator() && e.isPickable()
        );

        if (entityResult != null) {
            return entityResult;
        }

        // 縺ｪ縺代ｌ縺ｰ繝悶Ο繝・け蛻､螳・
        return player.pick(range, 0.0F, false);
    }

    // 繧｢繝九Γ繝ｼ繧ｷ繝ｧ繝ｳ (謇九↓謖√▲縺滓凾縺ｮ蜍輔″)
    @Override
    public UseAnim getUseAnimation(ItemStack stack) {
        return UseAnim.BOW;
    }

    @Override
    public int getUseDuration(ItemStack stack) {
        return 72000;
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.item;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.learning.CardinalLearningManager;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.main.CollaboMod;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.TextComponent;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResultHolder;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

public class ItemEvaluator extends Item {

    public ItemEvaluator() {
        super(new Item.Properties().tab(CollaboMod.COLLABOMOD_TAB).stacksTo(1));
    }

    @Override
    public InteractionResultHolder<ItemStack> use(Level level, Player player, InteractionHand hand) {
        ItemStack stack = player.getItemInHand(hand);

        if (!level.isClientSide) {
            // 迴ｾ蝨ｨ縺ｮ繧ｹ繧ｳ繧｢繧貞叙蠕・(繝・ヵ繧ｩ繝ｫ繝・轤ｹ)
            int currentScore = getScore(stack);

            // A. 繧ｹ繝九・繧ｯ + 蜿ｳ繧ｯ繝ｪ繝・け : 繧ｹ繧ｳ繧｢螟画峩 (繧ｵ繧､繧ｯ繝ｫ)
            if (player.isCrouching()) {
                currentScore++;
                if (currentScore > 10) currentScore = 1;
                setScore(stack, currentScore);

                // 繧｢繧ｯ繧ｷ繝ｧ繝ｳ繝舌・縺ｫ陦ｨ遉ｺ
                player.displayClientMessage(new TextComponent("ﾂｧe[隧穂ｾ｡險ｭ螳咯 " + getStarDisplay(currentScore)), true);
            }
            // B. 騾壼ｸｸ蜿ｳ繧ｯ繝ｪ繝・け : 隧穂ｾ｡騾∽ｿ｡
            else {
                // 繧ｹ繧ｳ繧｢繧・0.0 ~ 1.0 縺ｫ豁｣隕丞喧縺励※貂｡縺・(1轤ｹ=0.1, 10轤ｹ=1.0)
                // 窶ｻ 1轤ｹ縺ｪ縺ｩ縺ｮ菴手ｩ穂ｾ｡縺ｯ縲・.1縲阪→縺励※險倬鹸縺輔ｌ縲、I縺ｸ縺ｮ蠖ｱ髻ｿ蜉帙′讌ｵ繧√※蟆上＆縺上↑繧・
                // 繧ゅ＠縲御ｺ悟ｺｦ縺ｨ蜃ｺ縺吶↑縲阪→縺・≧蠑ｷ縺・凄螳壹ｒ縺励◆縺・ｴ蜷医・縲・轤ｹ縺ｮ縺ｨ縺阪□縺・-1.0 繧帝√ｋ繝ｭ繧ｸ繝・け縺ｫ縺励※繧り憶縺・

                float normalizedScore;
                if (currentScore == 1) {
                    normalizedScore = -1.0f; // 1轤ｹ縺ｯ縲悟ｮ悟・蜷ｦ螳夲ｼ育ｦ∵ｭ｢・峨阪→縺励※謇ｱ縺・
                } else {
                    normalizedScore = currentScore / 10.0f; // 2~10轤ｹ縺ｯ縲碁㍾縺ｿ縲阪→縺励※謇ｱ縺・
                }

                CardinalLearningManager.getInstance().rateLastInteraction(normalizedScore);

                String msg = (normalizedScore < 0) ? "ﾂｧc[AI蟄ｦ鄙綻 隧穂ｾ｡: Bad (髯､螟門ｯｾ雎｡)" : "ﾂｧa[AI蟄ｦ鄙綻 隧穂ｾ｡: " + currentScore + "轤ｹ 縺ｧ險倬鹸";
                player.sendMessage(new TextComponent(msg), player.getUUID());
            }
        }
        return InteractionResultHolder.success(stack);
    }

    // NBT繝倥Ν繝代・
    private int getScore(ItemStack stack) {
        CompoundTag tag = stack.getOrCreateTag();
        if (!tag.contains("Score")) tag.putInt("Score", 5);
        return tag.getInt("Score");
    }

    private void setScore(ItemStack stack, int score) {
        stack.getOrCreateTag().putInt("Score", score);
    }

    // 笘・↑縺ｩ縺ｮ譁・ｭ怜・陦ｨ迴ｾ
    private String getStarDisplay(int score) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.append("ﾂｧ6"); // 驥題牡
        for (int i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
            if (i <= score) sb.append("笘・);
            else sb.append("笘・);
        }
        sb.append(" ﾂｧf(").append(score).append("/10)");
        return sb.toString();
    }
}

==================================================
FILE_PATH: C:\mincraft-mod\CollaboMod\src\main\java\com\COLLABOMOD\collabomod\item\ItemSilverHorn.java
==================================================
package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.item;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.capability.MagicStatsProvider;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.entity.EntityMistDispersion;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.main.CollaboMod;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.util.PsionParticleUtil;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.world.idea.EidosData;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.world.idea.IdeaDimensionData;
import net.minecraft.Util;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.TextComponent;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResult;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResultHolder;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.TooltipFlag;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;

import java.util.List;

public class ItemSilverHorn extends Item implements ICAD{

    private final float hardwarePerformance = 2.0F;

    // 螳壽焚: 繝｢繝ｼ繝迂D
    private static final int MODE_DECOMPOSITION = 0;
    private static final int MODE_REGROWTH = 1;

    public ItemSilverHorn() {
        super(new Item.Properties().tab(CollaboMod.COLLABOMOD_TAB).stacksTo(1));
    }

    // 笆 繝・・繝ｫ繝√ャ繝暦ｼ医・繧ｦ繧ｹ繝帙ヰ繝ｼ譎ゅ↓繝｢繝ｼ繝峨ｒ陦ｨ遉ｺ・・
    @Override
    public void appendHoverText(ItemStack stack, @Nullable Level level, List<Component> tooltip, TooltipFlag flag) {
        int mode = getMode(stack);
        String modeName = (mode == MODE_DECOMPOSITION) ? "ﾂｧc[蛻・ｧ｣ - Mist Dispersion]" : "ﾂｧa[蜀肴・ - Regrowth]";
        tooltip.add(new TextComponent("襍ｷ蜍戊｡灘ｼ・ " + modeName));
        super.appendHoverText(stack, level, tooltip, flag);
    }

    // 笆 蜿ｳ繧ｯ繝ｪ繝・け・育ｩｺ謦・■ or 繝｢繝ｼ繝牙・譖ｿ・・
    @Override
    public InteractionResultHolder<ItemStack> use(Level level, Player player, InteractionHand hand) {
        ItemStack stack = player.getItemInHand(hand);

        // --- 繝｢繝ｼ繝牙・譖ｿ蜃ｦ逅・(Shift + 蜿ｳ繧ｯ繝ｪ繝・け) ---
        if (player.isCrouching()) {
            if (!level.isClientSide) {
                cycleMode(stack, player);
            }
            return InteractionResultHolder.success(stack);
        }

        // --- 鬲疲ｳ慕匱蜍募・逅・---
        int mode = getMode(stack);

        // 1. 蛻・ｧ｣繝｢繝ｼ繝峨・蝣ｴ蜷・-> 逋ｺ蟆・
        if (mode == MODE_DECOMPOSITION) {
            executeDecomposition(level, player);
            return InteractionResultHolder.success(stack);
        }

        // 2. 蜀肴・繝｢繝ｼ繝峨・蝣ｴ蜷・-> 遨ｺ謦・■荳榊庄・亥ｯｾ雎｡縺悟ｿ・ｦ・ｼ・
        else {
            if (!level.isClientSide) {
                player.sendMessage(new TextComponent("ﾂｧe蜀肴・繝｢繝ｼ繝・ 蟇ｾ雎｡縺ｫ蜷代￠縺ｦ菴ｿ逕ｨ縺励※縺上□縺輔＞"), Util.NIL_UUID);
            }
            return InteractionResultHolder.fail(stack);
        }
    }

    // 笆 繧ｨ繝ｳ繝・ぅ繝・ぅ縺ｸ縺ｮ蜿ｳ繧ｯ繝ｪ繝・け・亥・謌舌・逋ｺ蜍包ｼ・
    @Override
    public InteractionResult interactLivingEntity(ItemStack stack, Player player, LivingEntity target, InteractionHand hand) {
        int mode = getMode(stack);

        // 蜀肴・繝｢繝ｼ繝峨・譎ゅ□縺代√お繝ｳ繝・ぅ繝・ぅ縺ｫ蟇ｾ縺吶ｋ蜃ｦ逅・ｒ陦後≧
        if (mode == MODE_REGROWTH) {
            if (!player.level.isClientSide && player.level instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel) {
                castRegrowth(serverLevel, player, target);
            }
            return InteractionResult.SUCCESS;
        }

        // 蛻・ｧ｣繝｢繝ｼ繝峨↑繧我ｽ輔ｂ縺励↑縺・ｼ・se繝｡繧ｽ繝・ラ縺ｫ蜃ｦ逅・ｒ豬√＠縺ｦ謦・▽・・
        return InteractionResult.PASS;
    }

    // --- 繝倥Ν繝代・繝｡繧ｽ繝・ラ: NBT謫堺ｽ・---

    private int getMode(ItemStack stack) {
        CompoundTag tag = stack.getOrCreateTag();
        return tag.getInt("CADMode");
    }

    private void cycleMode(ItemStack stack, Player player) {
        CompoundTag tag = stack.getOrCreateTag();
        int currentMode = tag.getInt("CADMode");
        int newMode = (currentMode == MODE_DECOMPOSITION) ? MODE_REGROWTH : MODE_DECOMPOSITION;
        tag.putInt("CADMode", newMode);

        // 蛻・崛髻ｳ縺ｨ繝｡繝・そ繝ｼ繧ｸ
        player.level.playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY(), player.getZ(),
                SoundEvents.COMPARATOR_CLICK, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0F, 1.5F);

        String modeName = (newMode == MODE_DECOMPOSITION) ? "ﾂｧc襍ｷ蜍戊｡灘ｼ・ 蛻・ｧ｣ (Mist Dispersion)" : "ﾂｧa襍ｷ蜍戊｡灘ｼ・ 蜀肴・ (Regrowth)";
        player.displayClientMessage(new TextComponent(modeName), true); // 繧｢繧ｯ繧ｷ繝ｧ繝ｳ繝舌・縺ｫ陦ｨ遉ｺ
    }

    // --- 鬲疲ｳ輔Ο繧ｸ繝・け (蛻・ｧ｣) ---
    private void executeDecomposition(Level level, Player player) {
        // 繧ｵ繝ｼ繝舌・蛛ｴ
        if (!level.isClientSide) {
            player.getCapability(MagicStatsProvider.PLAYER_MAGIC_STATS).ifPresent(serverStats -> {
                int stress = serverStats.getMentalLoad();
                if (stress > 80) {
                    if (level.getRandom().nextInt(100) < (stress - 80) * 2) {
                        handleFizzle(level, player);
                        return;
                    }
                }

                int cost = 30;
                if (serverStats.getCurrentPsion() >= cost) {
                    serverStats.setCurrentPsion(serverStats.getCurrentPsion() - cost);
                    serverStats.addMentalLoad(4);

                    EntityMistDispersion magic = new EntityMistDispersion(level, player);
                    magic.shootFromRotation(player, player.getXRot(), player.getYRot(), 0.0F, 7.0F, 0.0F);
                    level.addFreshEntity(magic);

                    level.playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY(), player.getZ(),
                            SoundEvents.TRIDENT_RIPTIDE_1, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.5F, 2.5F);
                } else {
                    if (player.tickCount % 20 == 0) {
                        player.sendMessage(new TextComponent("諠ｳ蟄蝉ｸ崎ｶｳ"), Util.NIL_UUID);
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        // 繧ｯ繝ｩ繧､繧｢繝ｳ繝亥・・医ヱ繝ｼ繝・ぅ繧ｯ繝ｫ・・
        if (level.isClientSide) {
            player.getCapability(MagicStatsProvider.PLAYER_MAGIC_STATS).ifPresent(clientStats -> {
                if (clientStats.getCurrentPsion() >= 30) {
                    Vec3 look = player.getLookAngle();
                    Vec3 pos = player.getEyePosition().add(look.scale(1.0));
                    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                        float dist = 0.5F * i;
                        float radius = 0.2F + (0.2F * i);
                        PsionParticleUtil.spawnPsionRing(level, pos.add(look.scale(dist)), look, radius, 30);
                    }
                }
            });
        }
    }

    // --- 鬲疲ｳ輔Ο繧ｸ繝・け (蜀肴・) ---
    private void castRegrowth(ServerLevel level, Player player, LivingEntity target) {
        player.getCapability(MagicStatsProvider.PLAYER_MAGIC_STATS).ifPresent(rStats -> {
            IdeaDimensionData idea = IdeaDimensionData.get(level);
            EidosData backup = idea.getOptimalEntityState(target.getUUID());

            if (backup == null) {
                player.sendMessage(new TextComponent("ﾂｧc菫ｮ蠕ｩ蜿ｯ閭ｽ縺ｪ繧ｨ繧､繝峨せ縺悟ｭ伜惠縺励∪縺帙ｓ"), Util.NIL_UUID);
                return;
            }

            int cost = 100;
            if (rStats.getCurrentPsion() >= cost) {
                rStats.setCurrentPsion(rStats.getCurrentPsion() - cost);

                float currentHP = target.getHealth();
                // NBT縺九ｉ蜿門ｾ励＠縺櫞P・磯℃蜴ｻ縺ｮ譛螟ｧ縺ｮ迥ｶ諷具ｼ・
                float oldHP = backup.getEntityData().contains("Health") ? backup.getEntityData().getFloat("Health") : target.getMaxHealth();

                // 笆 縺薙％縺ｧ豈碑ｼ・ 繧ゅ＠迴ｾ蝨ｨ縺ｮHP縺後√ヰ繝・け繧｢繝・・縺ｮHP縺ｨ蜷後§縺九◎繧御ｻ･荳翫↑繧峨悟屓蠕ｩ縺ｮ蠢・ｦ√↑縺励・
                if (currentHP >= oldHP) {
                    player.sendMessage(new TextComponent("ﾂｧe蟇ｾ雎｡縺ｮ繧ｨ繧､繝峨せ縺ｯ譖ｸ縺肴鋤縺亥・譚･縺ｾ縺帙ｓ"), Util.NIL_UUID);
                    // 繧ｳ繧ｹ繝医ｒ霑秘ｄ縺励※繧ゅ＞縺・〒縺吶′縲∫匱蜍輔＠縺滓凾轤ｹ縺ｧ豸郁ｲｻ縺吶ｋ縺ｮ縺碁壻ｾ・
                    return;
                }

                float damageDiff = oldHP - currentHP;

                CompoundTag oldData = backup.getEntityData();
                net.minecraft.nbt.ListTag posList = new net.minecraft.nbt.ListTag();
                posList.add(net.minecraft.nbt.DoubleTag.valueOf(target.getX()));
                posList.add(net.minecraft.nbt.DoubleTag.valueOf(target.getY()));
                posList.add(net.minecraft.nbt.DoubleTag.valueOf(target.getZ()));
                oldData.put("Pos", posList);

                target.load(oldData);
                // load逶ｴ蠕後・菴咲ｽｮ諠・ｱ縺御ｸ榊ｮ牙ｮ壹↑蝣ｴ蜷医′縺ゅｋ縺溘ａ蜀阪そ繝・ヨ
                target.setPos(target.getX(), target.getY(), target.getZ());

                // 笆 菫ｮ豁｣: 蜀肴・逶ｴ蠕後・謾ｻ謦・愛螳壹ｒ髦ｲ縺舌◆繧√・遘帝俣・・0tick・峨・辟｡謨ｵ譎る俣繧剃ｻ倅ｸ・
                target.invulnerableTime = 20;

                // 笆 菫ｮ豁｣: 繧､繝・い縺九ｉ螻･豁ｴ繧堤｢ｺ螳溘↓豸亥悉縺励・｣邯壻ｽｿ逕ｨ繧剃ｸ榊庄縺ｫ縺吶ｋ
                idea.clearHistory(target.getUUID());

                level.playSound(null, target.getX(), target.getY(), target.getZ(),
                        SoundEvents.ZOMBIE_VILLAGER_CONVERTED, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0F, 1.5F);
                player.sendMessage(new TextComponent("ﾂｧb蜀肴・螳御ｺ・), Util.NIL_UUID);

                idea.clearHistory(target.getUUID());

                int painLoad = (int)(damageDiff * 2);
                if (painLoad < 10) painLoad = 10;
                rStats.addMentalLoad(painLoad);

                int duration = painLoad * 10;
                player.addEffect(new net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance(net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffects.BLINDNESS, duration, 0));
                player.addEffect(new net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance(net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffects.MOVEMENT_SLOWDOWN, duration, 4));
                player.addEffect(new net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance(net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffects.CONFUSION, duration, 0));

            } else {
                player.sendMessage(new TextComponent("諠ｳ蟄蝉ｸ崎ｶｳ"), Util.NIL_UUID);
            }
        });
    }

    private void handleFizzle(Level level, Player player) {
        level.playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY(), player.getZ(),
                SoundEvents.FIRE_EXTINGUISH, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0F, 1.5F);
        player.hurt(net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource.MAGIC, 2.0F);
        player.sendMessage(new TextComponent("ﾂｧc貍皮ｮ鈴≦蟒ｶ..."), Util.NIL_UUID);
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.item;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.client.ClientEvents;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.main.CollaboMod;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResultHolder;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;

public class ItemThirdEye extends Item implements ICAD{

    public ItemThirdEye() {
        super(new Item.Properties().tab(CollaboMod.COLLABOMOD_TAB).stacksTo(1));
    }

    @Override
    public InteractionResultHolder<ItemStack> use(Level level, Player player, InteractionHand hand) {
        // 繧ｵ繝ｼ繝舌・蛛ｴ縺ｯ菴輔ｂ縺励↑縺・ｼ医け繝ｩ繧､繧｢繝ｳ繝医°繧峨・蠎ｧ讓咎夂衍繧貞ｾ・▽・・
        if (!level.isClientSide) {
            return InteractionResultHolder.success(player.getItemInHand(hand));
        }

        // 繧ｯ繝ｩ繧､繧｢繝ｳ繝亥・: 繧ｨ繝ｬ繝｡繝ｳ繧ｿ繝ｫ繝ｻ繧ｵ繧､繝医ｒ縲後し繝ｼ繝峨・繧｢繧､繝｢繝ｼ繝峨阪〒襍ｷ蜍・
        // 窶ｻClientEvents縺ｮ繝｡繧ｽ繝・ラ繧堤峩謗･蜻ｼ縺ｳ蜃ｺ縺吝ｽ｢縺ｫ縺ｪ繧翫∪縺・
        ClientEvents.toggleThirdEyeMode();

        return InteractionResultHolder.success(player.getItemInHand(hand));
    }
}

FILE_PATH: C:\mincraft-mod\CollaboMod\src\main\java\com\COLLABOMOD\collabomod\learning\AnalysisEngine.java

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.learning;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.*;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.learning.AttributePreference.AttributeNode;
import com.mojang.math.Vector3f;

import java.util.*;

public class AnalysisEngine {

    private static final Random random = new Random();

    // 螻樊ｧ繧､繝ｳ繝・ャ繧ｯ繧ｹ螳夂ｾｩ
    private static final int FIRE = 0;
    private static final int ICE = 1;
    private static final int AIR = 2;
    private static final int EARTH = 3;
    private static final int FORCE = 4;
    private static final int LIFE = 5;

    public static VisualMetadata deriveVisualsFromAttributes(float[] attributes, long seed) {
        // null縺ｪ繧峨ム繝溘・繧呈ｸ｡縺呻ｼ医す繝翫Μ繧ｪ逕滓・逕ｨ・・
        return generateScenarioFromScript(null, attributes, seed);
    }


    // 笆 繧ｷ繝翫Μ繧ｪ逕滓・繝ｭ繧ｸ繝・け
    public static VisualMetadata generateScenarioFromScript(List<String> script, float[] totalAttributes, long seed) {
        Random rand = new Random(seed);
        VisualMetadata meta = new VisualMetadata();
        AttributePreference brain = CardinalLearningManager.getInstance().getBrain();

        // 1. 蜈ｨ菴薙・繝・・繝樊ｱｺ螳・(閼ｳ繧剃ｽｿ縺｣縺ｦ縲・ｭ疲ｳ募・菴薙・縲御ｻ｣陦ｨ逧・↑濶ｲ繝ｻ蠖｢縲阪ｒ豎ｺ繧√ｋ)
        // 縺薙ｌ縺ｯ繝舌ャ繧ｯ繝励Ο繝代ご繝ｼ繧ｷ繝ｧ繝ｳ縺ｧ蟄ｦ鄙偵＆繧後◆邨先棡縺悟渚譏縺輔ｌ繧・
        VisualMetadata theme = inferThemeFromAttributes(brain, totalAttributes, rand);

        meta.mainColor = theme.mainColor;
        meta.subColor = theme.subColor;
        meta.shape = theme.shape;
        meta.scale = 1.0f + rand.nextFloat() * 1.5f;
        meta.layerCount = 1 + rand.nextInt(3);
        meta.rotationSpeed = (rand.nextFloat()-0.5f) * 5.0f;
        meta.timeSpeed = 1.0f;

        // 2. 繧ｿ繧､繝繝ｩ繧､繝ｳ讒狗ｯ・
        meta.timeline = new ArrayList<>();

        // A. 蜃ｺ迴ｾ (t=0.0)
        meta.timeline.add(new VisualKeyframe(0.0f, theme.mainColor, theme.subColor, 0.1f, 1.0f, theme.shape, theme.isSpiky, theme.isWavy, theme.hasLightning));

        if (script != null && !script.isEmpty()) {
            float currentTime = 0.1f;
            float durationPerLine = 0.8f / script.size();

            for (String line : script) {
                String l = line.toLowerCase();

                // 陦後＃縺ｨ縺ｮ螻樊ｧ謗ｨ隲・(陦悟腰菴阪〒閼ｳ縺ｫ蝠上＞蜷医ｏ縺帙ｋ縺薙→繧ょ庄閭ｽ縺縺後∽ｻ雁屓縺ｯ繝・・繝槭ｒ繝吶・繧ｹ縺ｫ螟牙ｽ｢縺輔○繧・
                EnumMagicShape phaseShape = theme.shape;
                float phaseScale = 1.0f;
                Vector3f phaseColor = new Vector3f(theme.mainColor.x(), theme.mainColor.y(), theme.mainColor.z());

                // 譁・ц隗｣譫・
                if (l.contains("move") || l.contains("fly")) {
                    phaseShape = EnumMagicShape.RING; // 遘ｻ蜍輔・繝ｪ繝ｳ繧ｰ
                    phaseScale = 1.0f;
                } else if (l.contains("accel")) {
                    phaseShape = EnumMagicShape.CYLINDER; // 蜉騾溘・莨ｸ縺ｳ繧・
                    phaseScale = 0.8f;
                } else if (l.contains("explode") || l.contains("burst")) {
                    phaseShape = EnumMagicShape.SPHERE; // 辷・匱
                    phaseScale = 3.0f; // 蟾ｨ螟ｧ蛹・
                    phaseColor.add(0.5f, 0.5f, 0.5f); // 霈昴￥
                } else if (l.contains("wait")) {
                    phaseScale = 0.5f; // 蜿守ｸｮ
                }

                meta.timeline.add(new VisualKeyframe(currentTime, phaseColor, theme.subColor, phaseScale, 1.0f, phaseShape, theme.isSpiky, theme.isWavy, theme.hasLightning));
                currentTime += durationPerLine;
            }
        } else {
            // 繧ｹ繧ｯ繝ｪ繝励ヨ縺後↑縺・ｴ蜷医・繝・ヵ繧ｩ繝ｫ繝亥虚菴・(閹ｨ蠑ｵ)
            meta.timeline.add(new VisualKeyframe(0.5f, theme.mainColor, theme.subColor, 2.0f, 1.0f, theme.shape, theme.isSpiky, theme.isWavy, theme.hasLightning));
        }

        // B. 豸亥､ｱ (t=1.0)
        VisualKeyframe last = meta.timeline.get(meta.timeline.size()-1);
        meta.timeline.add(new VisualKeyframe(1.0f, last.mainColor, last.subColor, last.scale * 1.5f, 0.0f, last.shape, last.isSpiky, last.isWavy, last.hasLightning));

        return meta;
    }

    // 閼ｳ繧剃ｽｿ縺｣縺ｦ螻樊ｧ繝吶け繝医Ν縺九ｉ縲瑚ｦ九◆逶ｮ縲阪ｒ謗ｨ隲悶☆繧九・繝ｫ繝代・
    private static VisualMetadata inferThemeFromAttributes(AttributePreference brain, float[] attributes, Random rand) {
        VisualMetadata meta = new VisualMetadata();
        // ... (莉･蜑阪・ deriveVisualsFromAttributes 縺ｮ繝ｭ繧ｸ繝・け繧偵％縺薙↓遘ｻ讀・ ...
        // Brain縺系ull縺ｪ繧峨ョ繝輔か繝ｫ繝亥､繧定ｿ斐☆
        // 蜉驥榊ｹｳ蝮・〒濶ｲ繧・ｽ｢繧呈ｱｺ螳壹☆繧・
        // (繧ｳ繝ｼ繝蛾㍼縺ｮ驛ｽ蜷井ｸ翫∫怐逡･縺励∪縺吶′縲∝燕蝗槭・deriveVisualsFromAttributes縺ｮ荳ｭ霄ｫ縺ｨ蜷後§縺ｧ縺・

        // 邁｡譏灘ｮ溯｣・
        meta.mainColor = new Vector3f(1,1,1);
        meta.subColor = new Vector3f(0,0.5f,1);
        meta.shape = EnumMagicShape.SPHERE;
        // 螳滄圀縺ｫ縺ｯ縺薙％縺ｧ brain.nodes 繧偵Ν繝ｼ繝励＠縺ｦ濶ｲ繧呈ｱｺ螳壹＠縺ｦ縺上□縺輔＞

        return meta;
    }


    public static float[] analyzeScript(List<String> script) {
        float[] vector = new float[6];
        for (String line : script) {
            String l = line.toLowerCase();
            if (l.contains("fire") || l.contains("explode") || l.contains("burst")) vector[FIRE] += 1.0f;
            if (l.contains("ice") || l.contains("freeze") || l.contains("water"))   vector[ICE] += 1.0f;
            if (l.contains("air") || l.contains("fly") || l.contains("move") || l.contains("wind")) vector[AIR] += 1.0f;
            if (l.contains("rock") || l.contains("protect") || l.contains("defend") || l.contains("shield")) vector[EARTH] += 1.0f;
            if (l.contains("shoot") || l.contains("accel") || l.contains("gram"))  vector[FORCE] += 1.0f;
            if (l.contains("restore") || l.contains("heal") || l.contains("regrowth")) vector[LIFE] += 1.0f;
        }
        float sum = 0;
        for (float v : vector) sum += v;
        if (sum > 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < vector.length; i++) vector[i] /= sum;
        }
        return vector;
    }

    public static float calculateComplexity(List<String> script) { return (float) script.size(); }

    public static VisualMetadata predictVisuals(float[] attributes) {
        return deriveVisualsFromAttributes(attributes, random.nextLong()); // 荳譌ｦ縺薙％繧ゅせ繝槭・繝育函謌舌↓蟋碑ｭｲ・医・繝ｼ繧ｸ繝ｭ繧ｸ繝・け縺ｯ蠕後〒謌ｻ縺励※繧り憶縺・ｼ・
    }


    public static PhysicsMetadata derivePhysicsFromAttributes(float[] attributes, List<String> script) {
        PhysicsMetadata phy = new PhysicsMetadata();

        // 1. 螻樊ｧ縺ｫ繧医ｋ蝓ｺ遉弱せ繝・・繧ｿ繧ｹ螳夂ｾｩ
        // Fire: 鬮俶ｸｩ縲・ｫ倥お繝阪Ν繧ｮ繝ｼ縲∬ｻｽ驥・
        phy.temperature += attributes[0] * 2000.0F;
        phy.energy += attributes[0] * 50.0F;

        // Ice: 菴取ｸｩ縲∝崋菴薙∫｡ｬ縺・
        if (attributes[1] > 0.1F) {
            phy.temperature -= attributes[1] * 300.0F; // 邨ｶ蟇ｾ髮ｶ蠎ｦ縺ｸ
            phy.isSolid = true;
            phy.hardness += attributes[1] * 10.0F;
            phy.mass += attributes[1] * 5.0F;
        }

        // Air: 鬮倬溘∬ｻｽ驥・
        phy.velocity += attributes[2] * 2.0F;
        phy.mass += attributes[2] * 0.1F;

        // Earth: 驥阪＞縲∫｡ｬ縺・∝崋菴・
        if (attributes[3] > 0.1F) {
            phy.mass += attributes[3] * 20.0F;
            phy.hardness += attributes[3] * 20.0F;
            phy.isSolid = true;
        }

        // Force: 邏皮ｲ九お繝阪Ν繧ｮ繝ｼ縲・ｫ伜ｨ∝鴨
        phy.energy += attributes[4] * 100.0F;
        phy.velocity += attributes[4] * 1.0F;

        // Life: (莉雁屓縺ｯ迚ｩ逅・ム繝｡繝ｼ繧ｸ蟆代↑繧√∫音谿雁柑譫懊↑縺ｩ)

        // 2. 繧ｹ繧ｯ繝ｪ繝励ヨ繧ｭ繝ｼ繝ｯ繝ｼ繝峨↓繧医ｋ謖吝虚陬懈ｭ｣
        boolean hasMove = false;
        boolean hasExplode = false;

        for (String line : script) {
            String l = line.toLowerCase();
            if (l.contains("move") || l.contains("fly") || l.contains("accel")) {
                phy.velocity += 1.0F; // 蜉騾・
                hasMove = true;
            }
            if (l.contains("explode") || l.contains("burst")) {
                phy.forceType = PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.RADIAL;
                phy.energy *= 1.5F; // 螽∝鴨蠅・
                hasExplode = true;
            }
            if (l.contains("freeze") || l.contains("solid")) {
                phy.isSolid = true;
            }
            if (l.contains("heavy") || l.contains("gravity")) {
                phy.gravity = true;
                phy.mass *= 2.0F;
            }
        }

        // 3. ForceType縺ｮ譛邨よｱｺ螳・
        if (phy.forceType == PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.NONE) {
            if (hasMove) {
                phy.forceType = PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.DIRECTIONAL; // 遘ｻ蜍慕ｳｻ縺ｪ繧牙ｰ・・
            } else if (phy.isSolid) {
                phy.forceType = PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.FIELD; // 蝗ｺ菴薙↑繧芽ｨｭ鄂ｮ
            } else {
                phy.forceType = PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.RADIAL; // 繝・ヵ繧ｩ繝ｫ繝医・謾ｾ蟆・
            }
        }

        // 繝吶・繧ｹ繝ｩ繧､繝ｳ陬懈ｭ｣
        if (phy.temperature < 0) phy.temperature = 0; // 邨ｶ蟇ｾ髮ｶ蠎ｦ荳矩剞
        if (phy.mass < 0.1F) phy.mass = 0.1F;

        return phy;
    }


    private static void addWeightedColor(Vector3f base, Vector3f target, float weight) {
        base.add(target.x() * weight, target.y() * weight, target.z() * weight);
    }

    // 繝倥Ν繝代・: 驥阪∩莉倥″謚ｽ驕ｸ
    private static <T> T pickWeightedFromMap(Map<T, Float> scores, Random rand, T defaultValue) {
        if (scores == null || scores.isEmpty()) return defaultValue; // 遨ｺ縺ｪ繧峨ョ繝輔か繝ｫ繝亥､繧定ｿ斐☆

        Map<T, Float> weights = new HashMap<>();
        float total = 0;

        for (Map.Entry<T, Float> entry : scores.entrySet()) {
            if (entry.getKey() == null || entry.getValue() == null) continue;
            float w = (float) Math.exp(entry.getValue());
            weights.put(entry.getKey(), w);
            total += w;
        }

        if (total <= 0) return defaultValue; // 蜷郁ｨ医ぞ繝ｭ縺ｪ繧峨ョ繝輔か繝ｫ繝亥､

        float value = rand.nextFloat() * total;
        for (Map.Entry<T, Float> entry : weights.entrySet()) {
            value -= entry.getValue();
            if (value <= 0) return entry.getKey();
        }
        // 縺薙％縺ｫ譚･繧九％縺ｨ縺ｯ遞縺縺悟ｿｵ縺ｮ縺溘ａ
        return weights.keySet().iterator().next();
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.learning;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.EnumMagicAnimation;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.EnumMagicShape;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.VisualMetadata;
import com.mojang.math.Vector3f;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

public class AttributePreference {

    // 蜷・ｱ樊ｧ・・~5・峨＃縺ｨ縺ｮ蟄ｦ鄙偵ョ繝ｼ繧ｿ
    // [0:Fire, 1:Ice, 2:Air, 3:Earth, 4:Force, 5:Life]
    public final AttributeNode[] nodes = new AttributeNode[6];

    public AttributePreference() {
        // 蛻晄悄蛹厄ｼ壽怙蛻昴・逵溘▲逋ｽ・医∪縺溘・髢狗匱閠・ｮ夂ｾｩ縺ｮ繝・ヵ繧ｩ繝ｫ繝亥､繧貞・繧後※繧り憶縺・ｼ・
        for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
            nodes[i] = new AttributeNode();
            // 蛻晄悄繝舌う繧｢繧ｹ・医さ繝ｼ繝ｫ繝峨せ繧ｿ繝ｼ繝亥ｯｾ遲厄ｼ峨→縺励※縲∝ｰ代＠縺縺題牡蜻ｳ繧貞・繧後※縺翫￥
            // 縺薙ｌ縺後↑縺・→譛蛻昴・蜈ｨ驛ｨ縲碁ｻ偵阪°繧峨せ繧ｿ繝ｼ繝医↓縺ｪ縺｣縺ｦ縺励∪縺・◆繧・
            setDefaultBias(i, nodes[i]);
        }
    }

    // 蟄ｦ鄙抵ｼ壼・蜉・螻樊ｧ) 縺ｨ 蜃ｺ蜉・謠冗判) 縺ｨ 隧穂ｾ｡(繧ｹ繧ｳ繧｢) 繧貞女縺大叙繧翫∬┻繧呈峩譁ｰ縺吶ｋ
    public void train(float[] inputAttributes, VisualMetadata output, float score) {
        // 繧ｹ繧ｳ繧｢繧・-1.0 ~ 1.0 縺ｫ豁｣隕丞喧・・轤ｹ= -1.0, 5轤ｹ= 0.0, 10轤ｹ= 1.0・・
        // 5轤ｹ莉･荳九・縲悟ｿ倥ｌ繧具ｼ磯・婿蜷代∈蟄ｦ鄙抵ｼ峨阪・轤ｹ莉･荳翫・縲瑚ｦ壹∴繧九・
        float learningSignal = (score * 10.0f - 5.0f) / 5.0f;

        // 蟄ｦ鄙堤紫・井ｸ蠎ｦ縺ｫ縺ｩ繧後￥繧峨＞諤ｧ譬ｼ繧貞､峨∴繧九°・峨・.1縺上ｉ縺・′螯･蠖・
        float learningRate = 0.1f;

        for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
            float attrStrength = inputAttributes[i];
            if (attrStrength <= 0.0f) continue;

            // 縲後◎縺ｮ螻樊ｧ縺悟ｼｷ縺九▲縺溘阪°縺､縲瑚ｩ穂ｾ｡縺碁ｫ倥°縺｣縺溘榊ｴ蜷医√◎縺ｮ螻樊ｧ縺ｮ逅・Φ蜒上ｒ譖ｴ譁ｰ
            // 譖ｴ譁ｰ驥・= 蜈･蜉帛ｼｷ蠎ｦ * 蟄ｦ鄙剃ｿ｡蜿ｷ * 蟄ｦ鄙堤紫
            float delta = attrStrength * learningSignal * learningRate;

            if (Math.abs(delta) > 0.001f) {
                nodes[i].update(output, delta);
            }
        }
    }

    private void setDefaultBias(int index, AttributeNode node) {
        // 蛻晄悄迥ｶ諷九・螳夂ｾｩ・井ｻ･蜑阪・AnalysisEngine縺ｮswitch譁・↓逶ｸ蠖難ｼ・
        // 縺薙％繧貞ｮ夂ｾｩ縺励※縺翫￥縺ｨ縲梧怙蛻昴°繧峨≠繧狗ｨ句ｺｦ縺昴ｌ縺｣縺ｽ縺・咲憾諷九↓縺ｪ繧・
        switch (index) {
            case 0: node.idealMainColor = new Vector3f(1.0f, 0.2f, 0.0f); break; // Fire
            case 1: node.idealMainColor = new Vector3f(0.4f, 0.8f, 1.0f); break; // Ice
            case 2: node.idealMainColor = new Vector3f(0.2f, 1.0f, 0.6f); break; // Air
            // ... 莉悶ｂ蜷梧ｧ倥↓
            default: node.idealMainColor = new Vector3f(0.5f, 0.5f, 0.5f);
        }
    }

    // --- 蜀・Κ繧ｯ繝ｩ繧ｹ・・縺､縺ｮ螻樊ｧ縺ｫ蟇ｾ縺吶ｋ螂ｽ縺ｿ ---
    public static class AttributeNode {
        public Vector3f idealMainColor = new Vector3f(0.5f, 0.5f, 0.5f);
        public Vector3f idealSubColor = new Vector3f(0.5f, 0.5f, 0.5f);

        // 蜷・ヵ繝ｩ繧ｰ縺後卦rue縺ｧ縺ゅｋ縺ｹ縺阪阪せ繧ｳ繧｢ (-1.0 ~ 1.0)
        public float spikyScore = 0.0f;
        public float wavyScore = 0.0f;
        public float lightningScore = 0.0f;

        // 蠖｢迥ｶ繝ｻ繧｢繝九Γ繝ｼ繧ｷ繝ｧ繝ｳ縺ｮ螂ｽ縺ｿ・育ｴｯ遨阪せ繧ｳ繧｢・・
        public Map<EnumMagicShape, Float> shapeScores = new HashMap<>();
        public Map<EnumMagicAnimation, Float> animScores = new HashMap<>();

        public void update(VisualMetadata meta, float delta) {
            // 濶ｲ縺ｮ蟄ｦ鄙・(繝吶け繝医Ν陬憺俣)
            // positive縺ｪ繧峨◎縺ｮ濶ｲ縺ｫ霑代▼縺阪］egative縺ｪ繧蛾□縺悶°繧・
            lerpColor(idealMainColor, meta.mainColor, delta);
            lerpColor(idealSubColor, meta.subColor, delta);

            // 繝代Λ繝｡繝ｼ繧ｿ縺ｮ蟄ｦ鄙・
            if (meta.isSpiky) spikyScore += delta; else spikyScore -= delta;
            if (meta.isWavy) wavyScore += delta; else wavyScore -= delta;
            if (meta.hasLightning) lightningScore += delta; else lightningScore -= delta;

            // 蠖｢迥ｶ縺ｮ蟄ｦ鄙・
            shapeScores.put(meta.shape, shapeScores.getOrDefault(meta.shape, 0.0f) + delta);

            // 繧｢繝九Γ繝ｼ繧ｷ繝ｧ繝ｳ縺ｮ蟄ｦ鄙・
            animScores.put(meta.animationType, animScores.getOrDefault(meta.animationType, 0.0f) + delta);
        }

        private void lerpColor(Vector3f current, Vector3f target, float t) {
            // 蜊倡ｴ斐↑邱壼ｽ｢陬憺俣縺縺後》縺後・繧､繝翫せ縺ｮ蝣ｴ蜷医・縲碁・婿蜷代阪↓蜍輔￥
            // Clamp蜃ｦ逅・ｒ蜈･繧後※0~1縺ｮ遽・峇繧堤ｶｭ謖√☆繧・
            float nx = clamp(current.x() + (target.x() - current.x()) * t);
            float ny = clamp(current.y() + (target.y() - current.y()) * t);
            float nz = clamp(current.z() + (target.z() - current.z()) * t);
            current.set(nx, ny, nz);
        }

        private float clamp(float v) { return Math.max(0.0f, Math.min(1.0f, v)); }
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.learning;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.EnumMagicAnimation;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.EnumMagicShape;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.VisualMetadata;
import com.mojang.math.Vector3f;

import java.util.Random;

public class BrainPreTrainer {

    // 閼ｳ縺ｫ縲御ｸ闊ｬ蟶ｸ隴倥阪ｒ豕ｨ蜈･縺吶ｋ
    public static void preTrain(AttributePreference brain) {
        System.out.println("[Cardinal AI] Starting Pre-training...");

        // 轣ｫ螻樊ｧ縺ｮ繝医Ξ繝ｼ繝九Φ繧ｰ (1000蝗櫁ｩｦ陦檎嶌蠖・
        trainConcept(brain, new float[]{1,0,0,0,0,0}, new Vector3f(1.0f, 0.2f, 0.0f), EnumMagicShape.SPHERE, true, false, true);

        // 豌ｷ螻樊ｧ縺ｮ繝医Ξ繝ｼ繝九Φ繧ｰ
        trainConcept(brain, new float[]{0,1,0,0,0,0}, new Vector3f(0.4f, 0.8f, 1.0f), EnumMagicShape.CYLINDER, true, false, false);

        // 鬚ｨ螻樊ｧ縺ｮ繝医Ξ繝ｼ繝九Φ繧ｰ
        trainConcept(brain, new float[]{0,0,1,0,0,0}, new Vector3f(0.2f, 1.0f, 0.6f), EnumMagicShape.RING, false, true, false);

        // 蝨溷ｱ樊ｧ縺ｮ繝医Ξ繝ｼ繝九Φ繧ｰ
        trainConcept(brain, new float[]{0,0,0,1,0,0}, new Vector3f(1.0f, 0.6f, 0.2f), EnumMagicShape.SPHERE, false, false, false);

        // 蜉帛ｱ樊ｧ・育┌螻樊ｧ・峨・繝医Ξ繝ｼ繝九Φ繧ｰ
        trainConcept(brain, new float[]{0,0,0,0,1,0}, new Vector3f(0.0f, 0.5f, 1.0f), EnumMagicShape.SPHERE, false, false, true);

        // 蜻ｽ螻樊ｧ縺ｮ繝医Ξ繝ｼ繝九Φ繧ｰ
        trainConcept(brain, new float[]{0,0,0,0,0,1}, new Vector3f(1.0f, 0.4f, 0.8f), EnumMagicShape.RING, false, true, false);

        System.out.println("[Cardinal AI] Pre-training complete.");
    }

    private static void trainConcept(AttributePreference brain, float[] attrs, Vector3f color, EnumMagicShape shape, boolean spiky, boolean wavy, boolean lightning) {
        VisualMetadata meta = new VisualMetadata();
        meta.mainColor = color;
        meta.subColor = color; // 邁｡譏・
        meta.shape = shape;
        meta.isSpiky = spiky;
        meta.isWavy = wavy;
        meta.hasLightning = lightning;

        // 閼ｳ縺ｫ縲後％繧後′豁｣隗｣縺縲阪→謨吶∴霎ｼ繧 (蠑ｷ辜医↓: 10轤ｹ貅轤ｹ隧穂ｾ｡繧・0蝗樒ｹｰ繧願ｿ斐☆蜉ｹ譫・
        for(int i=0; i<50; i++) {
            brain.train(attrs, meta, 1.0f); // 1.0 = Max Score
        }
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.learning;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.GsonBuilder;
import com.google.gson.reflect.TypeToken; // 霑ｽ蜉
import net.minecraftforge.fml.loading.FMLPaths;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileReader;
import java.io.FileWriter;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.lang.reflect.Type; // 霑ｽ蜉
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator; // 霑ｽ蜉
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors; // 霑ｽ蜉

public class CardinalLearningManager {

    private static final CardinalLearningManager INSTANCE = new CardinalLearningManager();
    private final List<LearningData> dataset = new ArrayList<>();
    private final Gson gson = new GsonBuilder().setPrettyPrinting().create();
    private final Path preferencePath;
    private AttributePreference globalPreference;
    private LearningData pendingData = null;

    private CardinalLearningManager() {
        // 菫晏ｭ伜・・喞onfig/collabomod_brain.json
        this.preferencePath = FMLPaths.CONFIGDIR.get().resolve("collabomod_brain.json");
        loadBrain();
    }

    public static CardinalLearningManager getInstance() {
        return INSTANCE;
    }

    public void registerInteraction(LearningData data) {
        this.pendingData = data;
    }
    // 閼ｳ縺ｸ繧｢繧ｯ繧ｻ繧ｹ縺吶ｋ繝｡繧ｽ繝・ラ
    public AttributePreference getBrain() {
        return globalPreference;
    }

    public void rateLastInteraction(float score) {
        if (pendingData != null) {
            pendingData.developerScore = score;
            dataset.add(pendingData);
            globalPreference.train(pendingData.attributeVector, pendingData.visualSettings, score);
            saveBrain();
            System.out.println("[Cardinal AI] Data saved. Score: " + score + ", Total Data: " + dataset.size());
            pendingData = null;
        } else {
            System.out.println("[Cardinal AI] No pending interaction to rate.");
        }
    }



    // 笆 霑ｽ蜉: k-霑大ｍ豕墓､懃ｴ｢ (莨ｼ縺ｦ縺・ｋ豁｣隗｣繝・・繧ｿ繧呈爾縺・

    private void saveBrain() {
        try (FileWriter writer = new FileWriter(preferencePath.toFile())) {
            gson.toJson(globalPreference, writer);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private void loadBrain() {
        File file = preferencePath.toFile();
        if (file.exists()) {
            try (FileReader reader = new FileReader(file)) {
                globalPreference = gson.fromJson(reader, AttributePreference.class);
            } catch (IOException e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
            }
        }

        // 繝輔ぃ繧､繝ｫ縺後↑縺・√∪縺溘・隱ｭ縺ｿ霎ｼ縺ｿ螟ｱ謨玲凾縺ｯ譁ｰ隕丈ｽ懈・
        if (globalPreference == null) {
            globalPreference = new AttributePreference();
        }
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.learning;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.science.ScienceContext;

public class FeedbackLoop {

    /**
     * 鬲疲ｳ募ｮ溯｡悟ｾ後・迚ｩ逅・ｵ先棡繧定ｧ｣譫舌＠縲∝ｭｦ鄙偵ョ繝ｼ繧ｿ縺ｫ諠・ｱ繧剃ｻ伜刈繝ｻ陬懈ｭ｣縺吶ｋ
     */
    public static void analyzeResult(LearningData data, ScienceContext result) {
        if (data == null || result == null) return;

        // 1. 迚ｩ逅・樟雎｡縺ｮ險倬鹸 (繧ｳ繝ｳ繧ｽ繝ｼ繝ｫ蜃ｺ蜉帙〒遒ｺ隱・
        // 螳滄圀縺ｫ縺ｯ LearningData 縺ｫ縲檎黄逅・せ繧ｳ繧｢縲阪ヵ繧｣繝ｼ繝ｫ繝峨ｒ菴懊▲縺ｦ菫晏ｭ倥☆繧九・縺檎炊諠ｳ縺ｧ縺吶′縲・
        // 縺ｾ縺壹・縲碁幕逋ｺ閠・′隧穂ｾ｡縺吶ｋ髫帙・蜿り・ュ蝣ｱ縲阪→縺励※蜃ｦ逅・＠縺ｾ縺吶・

        float tempDiff = Math.abs(result.temperature - 300.0F);
        float energyUsed = result.energy;

        // 萓・ 莠域Φ莉･荳翫・繧ｨ繝阪Ν繧ｮ繝ｼ縺檎匱逕溘＠縺溷ｴ蜷医・繝ｭ繧ｰ
        if (energyUsed > 10000.0F) {
            // System.out.println("[Feedback] High Energy Event Detected.");
        }

        // 2. 閾ｪ蜍戊｣懈ｭ｣縺ｮ莠亥ｙ蜍穂ｽ・(蟆・擂逧・↑諡｡蠑ｵ逕ｨ)
        // 萓・ 縲檎・逋ｺ縲阪ち繧ｰ縺後≠繧九・縺ｫ蜊雁ｾ・′蟆上＆縺吶℃縺溷ｴ蜷医∵ｬ｡蝗槭∈縺ｮ繝壹リ繝ｫ繝・ぅ諠・ｱ繧定ｨ倬鹸縺吶ｋ縺ｪ縺ｩ
        // if (result.type == PhenomenonType.SPHERE_EXPANSION && result.radius < 2.0F) { ... }

        // 莉雁屓縺ｯ繧ｷ繝ｳ繝励Ν縺ｫ繝・・繧ｿ繧貞女縺大叙繧九□縺代→縺吶ｋ
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.learning;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.VisualMetadata;
import com.mojang.math.Vector3f;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;

public class LearningData {
    // --- 蜈･蜉帷音蠕ｴ驥・(Context) ---
    public int scriptHash;           // 鬲疲ｳ募ｼ上・繝上ャ繧ｷ繝･
    public float[] attributeVector;  // [轣ｫ, 豌ｷ, 鬚ｨ, 蝨・ 蜈・ 髣Ⅹ 縺ｪ縺ｩ縺ｮ螻樊ｧ蠑ｷ蠎ｦ
    public float complexity;         // 鬲疲ｳ募ｼ上・隍・尅蠎ｦ

    // --- 蜃ｺ蜉幄ｨｭ螳・(Action) ---
    public VisualMetadata visualSettings;

    // --- 隧穂ｾ｡ (Reward) ---
    // 髢狗匱閠・↓繧医ｋ謇句虚隧穂ｾ｡繧ｹ繧ｳ繧｢ (-1.0: Bad, 0.0: Neutral, 1.0: Good)
    public float developerScore;

    public LearningData() {
        this.visualSettings = new VisualMetadata();
        this.attributeVector = new float[6];
    }

    public LearningData(int hash, float[] attributes, float complexity, VisualMetadata visuals) {
        this.scriptHash = hash;
        this.attributeVector = attributes;
        this.complexity = complexity;
        this.visualSettings = visuals;
        this.developerScore = 0.0f;
    }

    // --- 邁｡譏薙す繝ｪ繧｢繝ｩ繧､繧ｺ (譛ｬ譚･縺ｯGSON遲峨ｒ菴ｿ縺・′縲¨BT縺ｧ莉｣逕ｨ繧ょ庄) ---
    // 縺薙％縺ｧ縺ｯ讒矩縺ｮ螳夂ｾｩ縺ｮ縺ｿ
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic;

import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;

public class PhysicsMetadata {
    // 蝓ｺ譛ｬ迚ｩ逅・㍼
    public float temperature = 300.0F; // 貂ｩ蠎ｦ (繧ｱ繝ｫ繝薙Φ)
    public float mass = 1.0F;          // 雉ｪ驥・
    public float velocity = 0.0F;      // 蛻晞・
    public float hardness = 0.0F;      // 遑ｬ蠎ｦ (繧ｷ繝ｼ繝ｫ繝牙ｼｷ蠎ｦ繧・ｲｫ騾壼鴨)
    public float energy = 0.0F;        // 邏皮ｲ九お繝阪Ν繧ｮ繝ｼ驥・(繝繝｡繝ｼ繧ｸ險育ｮ礼畑)

    // 謖吝虚繝輔Λ繧ｰ
    public boolean isSolid = false;    // 迚ｩ逅・噪縺ｪ蠖薙◆繧雁愛螳壹ｒ謖√▽縺・
    public boolean isHoming = false;   // 隱伜ｰ弱☆繧九°
    public boolean gravity = false;    // 驥榊鴨縺ｮ蠖ｱ髻ｿ繧貞女縺代ｋ縺・

    // 蜉帙・諤ｧ雉ｪ
    public EnumForceType forceType = EnumForceType.NONE;

    public enum EnumForceType {
        NONE,
        RADIAL,      // 謾ｾ蟆・憾 (辷・匱)
        DIRECTIONAL, // 謖・髄諤ｧ (蟆・茶)
        FIELD        // 鬆伜沺 (險ｭ鄂ｮ)
    }

    public PhysicsMetadata() {}

    public CompoundTag toNBT() {
        CompoundTag tag = new CompoundTag();
        tag.putFloat("Temp", temperature);
        tag.putFloat("Mass", mass);
        tag.putFloat("Vel", velocity);
        tag.putFloat("Hard", hardness);
        tag.putFloat("Energy", energy);
        tag.putBoolean("Solid", isSolid);
        tag.putBoolean("Homing", isHoming);
        tag.putBoolean("Grav", gravity);
        tag.putInt("FType", forceType.ordinal());
        return tag;
    }

    public static PhysicsMetadata fromNBT(CompoundTag tag) {
        PhysicsMetadata meta = new PhysicsMetadata();
        if (tag.contains("Temp")) meta.temperature = tag.getFloat("Temp");
        if (tag.contains("Mass")) meta.mass = tag.getFloat("Mass");
        if (tag.contains("Vel")) meta.velocity = tag.getFloat("Vel");
        if (tag.contains("Hard")) meta.hardness = tag.getFloat("Hard");
        if (tag.contains("Energy")) meta.energy = tag.getFloat("Energy");
        if (tag.contains("Solid")) meta.isSolid = tag.getBoolean("Solid");
        if (tag.contains("Homing")) meta.isHoming = tag.getBoolean("Homing");
        if (tag.contains("Grav")) meta.gravity = tag.getBoolean("Grav");
        if (tag.contains("FType")) meta.forceType = EnumForceType.values()[tag.getInt("FType")];
        return meta;
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.science.ScienceContext;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class SpellContext {
    public Level level;
    public LivingEntity caster;
    public LivingEntity target;
    public Vec3 origin;

    // 繧ｹ繧ｯ繝ｪ繝励ヨ諠・ｱ
    public List<String> script = new ArrayList<>();

    // 笆 AI謗ｨ隲也ｵ先棡
    public VisualMetadata visuals = new VisualMetadata();
    public PhysicsMetadata physics = new PhysicsMetadata(); // 霑ｽ蜉

    // 譌ｧ繧ｷ繧ｹ繝・Β莠呈鋤逕ｨ (蠕舌・↓蟒・ｭ｢)
    public int cost = 0;
    public int castTime = 0;
    public boolean fromSequence = false;
    public ScienceContext science = new ScienceContext(); // 迚ｩ逅・ｼ皮ｮ礼畑繧ｳ繝ｳ繝・く繧ｹ繝・

    public SpellContext() {}
}
