FILE_PATH: C:\mincraft-mod\CollaboMod\src\main\java\com\COLLABOMOD\collabomod\magic\SpellExecutor.java
==================================================
package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.entity.EntityMagicSequence;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.entity.EntitySciencePhenomenon;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.learning.AnalysisEngine;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.learning.CardinalLearningManager;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.learning.LearningData;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

public class SpellExecutor {

    public static void execute(SpellContext ctx) {
        if (ctx.level.isClientSide) return;

        // 1. AI縺ｫ繧医ｋ繝代Λ繝｡繝ｼ繧ｿ謗ｨ隲・(譛ｪ險ｭ螳壹・蝣ｴ蜷・
        float[] attrVec = AnalysisEngine.analyzeScript(ctx.script);

        if (ctx.physics.forceType == PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.NONE) {
            ctx.physics = AnalysisEngine.derivePhysicsFromAttributes(attrVec, ctx.script);
        }

        long seed = ctx.script != null ? ctx.script.hashCode() : System.nanoTime();
        if (ctx.visuals.timeline.isEmpty()) {
            ctx.visuals = AnalysisEngine.deriveVisualsFromAttributes(attrVec, seed);
        }

        // 蟄ｦ鄙偵ョ繝ｼ繧ｿ縺ｮ險倬鹸貅門ｙ
        LearningData learningData = new LearningData(
                (int)seed, attrVec, ctx.script.size(), ctx.visuals
        );

        // 2. 螳溯｡悟・蟯・ 驕・ｻｶ逋ｺ蜍・(Sequence) 縺・蜊ｳ譎ら匱蜍・(Phenomenon) 縺・

        if (ctx.castTime > 0 && !ctx.fromSequence) {
            // --- A. 驕・ｻｶ逋ｺ蜍・(EntityMagicSequence) ---
            EntityMagicSequence sequence = new EntityMagicSequence(
                    ctx.level,
                    ctx.caster,
                    ctx.script,
                    ctx.physics,
                    ctx.visuals,
                    ctx.castTime
            );

            // 笆 菫ｮ豁｣邂・園: setLocation 縺ｧ縺ｯ縺ｪ縺・moveTo 繧剃ｽｿ逕ｨ
            Vec3 pos = ctx.origin != null ? ctx.origin : ctx.caster.position();
            float yRot = ctx.caster != null ? ctx.caster.getYRot() : 0.0F;
            float xRot = ctx.caster != null ? ctx.caster.getXRot() : 0.0F;

            sequence.moveTo(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z, yRot, xRot);

            ctx.level.addFreshEntity(sequence);

            // 隧蜚ｱ髢句ｧ矩浹
            ctx.level.playSound(null, pos.x, pos.y, pos.z,
                    SoundEvents.ENCHANTMENT_TABLE_USE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0F, 0.5F);

        } else {
            // --- B. 蜊ｳ譎ら匱蜍・(EntitySciencePhenomenon) ---
            EntitySciencePhenomenon phenomenon = new EntitySciencePhenomenon(ctx.level, ctx.caster);

            phenomenon.setPhysicsMetadata(ctx.physics);
            phenomenon.setVisualMetadata(ctx.visuals);

            // 蜃ｺ迴ｾ菴咲ｽｮ縺ｮ豎ｺ螳・
            Vec3 spawnPos = ctx.origin;
            // 繧ｿ繝ｼ繧ｲ繝・ヨ謖・ｮ壹ｄ蟆・・邉ｻ縺ｮ蝣ｴ蜷医・菴咲ｽｮ隱ｿ謨ｴ
            if (ctx.physics.forceType == PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.DIRECTIONAL && ctx.caster != null) {
                spawnPos = ctx.caster.getEyePosition().add(ctx.caster.getLookAngle().scale(0.5));
            } else if (ctx.target != null) {
                spawnPos = ctx.target.position().add(0, 0.5, 0);
            } else if (spawnPos == null && ctx.caster != null) {
                spawnPos = ctx.caster.position();
            }

            if (spawnPos != null) {
                phenomenon.setPos(spawnPos);
            }

            // 蛻晞溘・驕ｩ逕ｨ (Directional縺ｪ繧牙髄縺・※縺・ｋ譁ｹ蜷代∈)
            if (ctx.physics.forceType == PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.DIRECTIONAL && ctx.caster != null) {
                Vec3 look = ctx.caster.getLookAngle();
                phenomenon.setDeltaMovement(look.scale(Math.max(0.5, ctx.physics.velocity)));
            }

            ctx.level.addFreshEntity(phenomenon);

            // 逋ｺ蜍暮浹
            if (spawnPos != null) {
                ctx.level.playSound(null, spawnPos.x, spawnPos.y, spawnPos.z,
                        SoundEvents.ILLUSIONER_CAST_SPELL, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0F, 1.0F);
            }

            // 繝輔ぅ繝ｼ繝峨ヰ繝・け逋ｻ骭ｲ (蜊ｳ譎ら匱蜍墓凾縺ｮ縺ｿ險倬鹸)
            CardinalLearningManager.getInstance().registerInteraction(learningData);
        }
    }
}

//package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic;

FILE_PATH: C:\mincraft-mod\CollaboMod\src\main\java\com\COLLABOMOD\collabomod\magic\SpellResolver.java
==================================================
package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.learning.AnalysisEngine;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.science.ScienceEngine;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ArrayList;

public class SpellResolver {

    /**
     * 繧ｹ繧ｯ繝ｪ繝励ヨ縺九ｉ鬲疲ｳ輔・繧ｳ繝ｳ繝・く繧ｹ繝茨ｼ育黄逅・・隕冶ｦ壼性繧・峨ｒ螳悟・隗｣豎ｺ縺吶ｋ
     */
    public static SpellContext resolve(List<String> scriptCode) {
        SpellContext ctx = new SpellContext();

        // 繧ｹ繧ｯ繝ｪ繝励ヨ縺ｮ繧ｻ繝・ヨ・・ull螳牙・・・
        ctx.script = scriptCode != null ? scriptCode : new ArrayList<>();

        // 1. 繧ｹ繧ｯ繝ｪ繝励ヨ隗｣譫・(迚ｹ蠕ｴ驥上・繧ｯ繝医Ν縺ｮ謚ｽ蜃ｺ)
        float[] attributes = AnalysisEngine.analyzeScript(ctx.script);

        // 2. 笆 迚ｩ逅・ヱ繝ｩ繝｡繝ｼ繧ｿ縺ｮ謗ｨ隲・(Physics Inference)
        // AI縺ｫ縲後％縺ｮ鬲疲ｳ輔・縺ｩ繧薙↑迚ｩ逅・樟雎｡縺具ｼ溘阪ｒ豎ｺ螳壹＆縺帙…tx.physics縺ｫ繧ｻ繝・ヨ縺吶ｋ
        ctx.physics = AnalysisEngine.derivePhysicsFromAttributes(attributes, ctx.script);

        // 3. 隕冶ｦ壹ヱ繝ｩ繝｡繝ｼ繧ｿ縺ｮ謗ｨ隲・(Visual Inference)
        // 迚ｩ逅・樟雎｡縺ｫ蜷医ｏ縺帙◆隕九◆逶ｮ繧呈ｱｺ螳壹☆繧・
        // Seed縺ｫ縺ｯ繧ｹ繧ｯ繝ｪ繝励ヨ縺ｮ繝上ャ繧ｷ繝･繧剃ｽｿ逕ｨ・亥酔縺伜測譁・↑繧牙ｸｸ縺ｫ蜷後§隕九◆逶ｮ繝ｻ迚ｩ逅・↓縺ｪ繧具ｼ・
        long seed = ctx.script.hashCode();

        // AI縺ｫ繧医ｋ繝・じ繧､繝ｳ逕滓・
        ctx.visuals = AnalysisEngine.deriveVisualsFromAttributes(attributes, seed);

        // 4. 迚ｩ逅・→隕九◆逶ｮ縺ｮ謨ｴ蜷域ｧ隱ｿ謨ｴ (Phys-Vis Sync)
        // AI縺梧ｱｺ繧√◆迚ｩ逅・㍼縺ｫ蜷医ｏ縺帙※縲∬ｦ九◆逶ｮ縺ｮ繧ｹ繧ｱ繝ｼ繝ｫ縺ｪ縺ｩ繧貞ｾｮ隱ｿ謨ｴ縺吶ｋ

        // 萓・ 雉ｪ驥上′螟ｧ縺阪＞縺ｪ繧峨∬ｦ九◆逶ｮ繧ょ､ｧ縺阪￥縺吶ｋ
        if (ctx.physics.mass > 1.0f) {
            ctx.visuals.scale = Math.max(ctx.visuals.scale, ctx.physics.mass * 0.5f);
        }

        // 萓・ 貂ｩ蠎ｦ縺碁ｫ倥＞縺ｪ繧峨∫匱蜈牙ｼｷ蠎ｦ・・ntensity・峨ｒ荳翫￡繧・
        if (ctx.physics.temperature > 1000.0f) {
            ctx.visuals.normalizedIntensity = 1.0f;
        }

        // 譌ｧ繧ｷ繧ｹ繝・Β・・cienceEngine・峨→縺ｮ莠呈鋤諤ｧ縺悟ｿ・ｦ√↑蝣ｴ蜷医・縺薙％縺ｧ騾｣謳ｺ
        // ScienceEngine.simulateVisuals(ctx.science, (int)seed);

        return ctx;
    }

    // 譌｢蟄倥さ繝ｼ繝峨→縺ｮ莠呈鋤諤ｧ邯ｭ謖∫畑・・isualMetadata縺縺第ｬｲ縺励＞蝣ｴ蜷茨ｼ・
    public static VisualMetadata resolveVisuals(List<String> scriptCode) {
        return resolve(scriptCode).visuals;
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic;

import com.mojang.math.Vector3f;

public class VisualKeyframe {
    public float timeStamp; // 0.0 (髢句ｧ・ ~ 1.0 (邨ゆｺ・

    public Vector3f mainColor;
    public Vector3f subColor;
    public float scale;
    public float alpha;

    public EnumMagicShape shape;
    public boolean isSpiky;
    public boolean isWavy;
    public boolean hasLightning;

    public VisualKeyframe(float time, Vector3f main, Vector3f sub, float scale, float alpha, EnumMagicShape shape, boolean spiky, boolean wavy, boolean lightning) {
        this.timeStamp = time;
        this.mainColor = main;
        this.subColor = sub;
        this.scale = scale;
        this.alpha = alpha;
        this.shape = shape;
        this.isSpiky = spiky;
        this.isWavy = wavy;
        this.hasLightning = lightning;
    }
}

FILE_PATH: C:\mincraft-mod\CollaboMod\src\main\java\com\COLLABOMOD\collabomod\magic\VisualMetadata.java
==================================================
package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic;

import com.mojang.math.Vector3f;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.ListTag;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class VisualMetadata {
    public EnumMagicShape shape = EnumMagicShape.RING;
    public EnumMagicAnchor anchorType = EnumMagicAnchor.WORLD_FIXED;
    public int priority = 0;
    public Vector3f mainColor = new Vector3f(1.0F, 1.0F, 1.0F);
    public Vector3f subColor = new Vector3f(0.0F, 0.5F, 1.0F);

    public float scale = 1.0F;
    public float density = 1.0F;
    public float rotationSpeed = 1.0F;

    // 笆 霑ｽ蜉: 繝励Ο繧ｷ繝ｼ繧ｸ繝｣繝ｫ逕滓・逕ｨ繝代Λ繝｡繝ｼ繧ｿ
    public boolean isWavy = false;       // 謖ｯ蜍募ｱ樊ｧ縺ｪ縺ｩ (豕｢謇薙▽)
    public boolean isSpiky = false;      // 諡｡謨｣繝ｻ謾ｻ謦・↑縺ｩ (繝医ご繝医ご)
    public int layerCount = 1;           // 繝ｬ繧､繝､繝ｼ謨ｰ (螽∝鴨縺ｫ蠢懊§縺ｦ蠅怜刈)

    public boolean hasLightning = false;
    public boolean isSolid = false;
    public String rendererID = "default";

    public int scriptHash = 0;
    public float normalizedIntensity = 0.0F;
    public float entropy = 0.0F;

    public EnumMagicAnimation animationType = EnumMagicAnimation.EXPAND_FADE;
    public float timeSpeed = 1.0F;
    public List<VisualKeyframe> timeline = new ArrayList<>();

    public VisualMetadata() {}

    public VisualMetadata(String rendererID, int priority, Vector3f color, float scale) {
        this.rendererID = rendererID;
        this.priority = priority;
        this.mainColor = color;
        this.scale = scale;
    }

    public void merge(VisualMetadata other) {
        if (other.priority > this.priority) {
            this.rendererID = other.rendererID;
            this.shape = other.shape;
            this.priority = other.priority;
            this.scale = other.scale;
            this.hasLightning = other.hasLightning;
            // 譁ｰ隕上ヱ繝ｩ繝｡繝ｼ繧ｿ縺ｮ繝槭・繧ｸ
            this.anchorType = other.anchorType;
            this.isWavy = other.isWavy || this.isWavy;
            this.isSpiky = other.isSpiky || this.isSpiky;
            this.layerCount = Math.max(this.layerCount, other.layerCount);
        }
        this.mainColor.add(other.mainColor);
        this.mainColor.mul(0.5F);
    }

    public CompoundTag toNBT() {
        CompoundTag tag = new CompoundTag();
        tag.putString("RendererID", rendererID);
        tag.putInt("Shape", shape.ordinal());
        tag.putInt("Anchor", anchorType.ordinal()); // 菫晏ｭ・
        tag.putFloat("ColorR", mainColor.x());
        tag.putFloat("ColorG", mainColor.y());
        tag.putFloat("ColorB", mainColor.z());
        tag.putFloat("Scale", scale);
        tag.putBoolean("IsWavy", isWavy);
        tag.putBoolean("IsSpiky", isSpiky);
        tag.putInt("Layers", layerCount);

        tag.putInt("ScriptHash", scriptHash);
        tag.putFloat("NormIntensity", normalizedIntensity);
        tag.putFloat("Entropy", entropy);
        tag.putInt("AnimType", animationType.ordinal());
        tag.putFloat("TimeSpeed", timeSpeed);

        // 繧ｿ繧､繝繝ｩ繧､繝ｳ菫晏ｭ・
        ListTag tList = new ListTag();
        for (VisualKeyframe k : timeline) {
            CompoundTag kTag = new CompoundTag();
            kTag.putFloat("T", k.timeStamp);
            kTag.putFloat("R", k.mainColor.x());
            kTag.putFloat("G", k.mainColor.y());
            kTag.putFloat("B", k.mainColor.z());
            kTag.putFloat("S", k.scale);
            kTag.putFloat("A", k.alpha);
            kTag.putInt("Shp", k.shape.ordinal());
            kTag.putBoolean("Spk", k.isSpiky);
            kTag.putBoolean("Wav", k.isWavy);
            kTag.putBoolean("Lit", k.hasLightning);
            tList.add(kTag);
        }
        tag.put("Timeline", tList);
        return tag;
    }

    public static VisualMetadata fromNBT(CompoundTag tag) {
        VisualMetadata meta = new VisualMetadata();
        if (tag.contains("RendererID")) meta.rendererID = tag.getString("RendererID");
        if (tag.contains("Shape")) meta.shape = EnumMagicShape.values()[tag.getInt("Shape")];
        if (tag.contains("Anchor")) meta.anchorType = EnumMagicAnchor.values()[tag.getInt("Anchor")];
        if (tag.contains("ColorR")) {
            meta.mainColor = new Vector3f(tag.getFloat("ColorR"), tag.getFloat("ColorG"), tag.getFloat("ColorB"));
        }
        if (tag.contains("Scale")) meta.scale = tag.getFloat("Scale");
        if (tag.contains("IsWavy")) meta.isWavy = tag.getBoolean("IsWavy");
        if (tag.contains("IsSpiky")) meta.isSpiky = tag.getBoolean("IsSpiky");
        if (tag.contains("Layers")) meta.layerCount = tag.getInt("Layers");

        if (tag.contains("ScriptHash")) meta.scriptHash = tag.getInt("ScriptHash");
        if (tag.contains("NormIntensity")) meta.normalizedIntensity = tag.getFloat("NormIntensity");
        if (tag.contains("Entropy")) meta.entropy = tag.getFloat("Entropy");
        if (tag.contains("AnimType")) meta.animationType = EnumMagicAnimation.values()[tag.getInt("AnimType")];
        if (tag.contains("TimeSpeed")) meta.timeSpeed = tag.getFloat("TimeSpeed");

        if (tag.contains("Timeline")) {
            ListTag tList = tag.getList("Timeline", 10);
            for (int i = 0; i < tList.size(); i++) {
                CompoundTag kTag = tList.getCompound(i);
                meta.timeline.add(new VisualKeyframe(
                        kTag.getFloat("T"),
                        new Vector3f(kTag.getFloat("R"), kTag.getFloat("G"), kTag.getFloat("B")),
                        new Vector3f(0,0,0), // subColor縺ｯ邁｡譏・
                        kTag.getFloat("S"),
                        kTag.getFloat("A"),
                        EnumMagicShape.values()[kTag.getInt("Shp")],
                        kTag.getBoolean("Spk"),
                        kTag.getBoolean("Wav"),
                        kTag.getBoolean("Lit")
                ));
            }
        }
        return meta;
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.main;


import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.client.KeyInit;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.client.particle.MagicGlowParticle;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.register.EntityRegister;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.register.ParticleRegister;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.EntityRenderers;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.client.renderer.RenderMagicSequence;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.client.renderer.RenderUniversalMagic;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.NoopRenderer;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.MenuScreens;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.register.MenuTypeRegister;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.client.gui.MagicConsoleScreen;
import net.minecraftforge.api.distmarker.Dist;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.register.ParticleRegister;
import net.minecraftforge.client.event.ParticleFactoryRegisterEvent;

import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.event.lifecycle.FMLClientSetupEvent;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;

@Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid = CollaboMod.MOD_ID, bus = Mod.EventBusSubscriber.Bus.MOD, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public class ClientEventBusSubscriber {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onClientSetup(FMLClientSetupEvent event) {
            // 繧ｰ繝ｩ繝繝ｻ繝・Δ繝ｪ繝・す繝ｧ繝ｳ縺ｯ繝｢繝・Ν繧呈戟縺溘★縲√ヱ繝ｼ繝・ぅ繧ｯ繝ｫ縺縺代〒陦ｨ迴ｾ縺吶ｋ縺溘ａ
            // "NoopRenderer"・井ｽ輔ｂ縺励↑縺・Ξ繝ｳ繝繝ｩ繝ｼ・晞乗・・峨ｒ蜑ｲ繧雁ｽ薙※縺ｾ縺・
            EntityRenderers.register(EntityRegister.GRAM_DEMOLITION.get(), NoopRenderer::new);
            EntityRenderers.register(EntityRegister.MIST_DISPERSION.get(), NoopRenderer::new);
            EntityRenderers.register(EntityRegister.AIR_BULLET.get(), NoopRenderer::new);
            EntityRenderers.register(EntityRegister.MAGIC_SEQUENCE.get(), RenderMagicSequence::new);
            EntityRenderers.register(EntityRegister.MAGIC_SEQUENCE.get(), RenderMagicSequence::new);
            EntityRenderers.register(EntityRegister.SCIENCE_PHENOMENON.get(), RenderUniversalMagic::new);
            KeyInit.register();

        event.enqueueWork(() -> {
            MenuScreens.register(MenuTypeRegister.MAGIC_CONSOLE_MENU.get(), MagicConsoleScreen::new);
        });
        }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void registerParticleFactories(ParticleFactoryRegisterEvent event) {
        Minecraft.getInstance().particleEngine.register(
                ParticleRegister.GLOW_PARTICLE.get(),
                MagicGlowParticle.Provider::new
        );
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.main;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.command.PsionCommand;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.register.*;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.EntityRenderers;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.NoopRenderer;
import net.minecraftforge.event.RegisterCommandsEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.event.lifecycle.FMLClientSetupEvent;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.main.tab.CollaboModBlockTab;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.main.tab.CollaboModTab;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.network.NetworkHandler;
import net.minecraft.world.item.CreativeModeTab;
import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.IEventBus;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.event.lifecycle.FMLCommonSetupEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.javafmlmod.FMLJavaModLoadingContext;

@Mod("collabo_mod")
public class CollaboMod {
    //Mod_ID
    public static final String MOD_ID = "collabo_mod";
    //繧ｯ繝ｪ繧ｨ繧､繝・ぅ繝悶ち繝悶・逋ｻ骭ｲ
    public static final CreativeModeTab COLLABOMOD_TAB = new CollaboModTab();
    public static final CreativeModeTab COLLABOMOD_BLOCK_TAB = new CollaboModBlockTab();

    public CollaboMod(){
        IEventBus eventBus = FMLJavaModLoadingContext.get().getModEventBus();

        eventBus.addListener(this::commonSetup);
        //縺薙・荳九↓霑ｽ蜉縺励※縺・￥

        //繧｢繧､繝・Β縺ｮ逋ｻ骭ｲ
        ItemRegister.register(eventBus);
        //繝悶Ο繝・け縺ｮ逋ｻ骭ｲ
        BlockRegister.register(eventBus);
        NetworkHandler.register();
        EntityRegister.register(eventBus);
        BlockEntityRegister.register(eventBus);
        MenuTypeRegister.register(eventBus);
        ParticleRegister.register(eventBus);

    }

    private void commonSetup(final FMLCommonSetupEvent event) {

    }
    private void onRegisterCommands(RegisterCommandsEvent event) {
        PsionCommand.register(event.getDispatcher());
    }

}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.network;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.main.CollaboMod;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkRegistry;
import net.minecraftforge.network.simple.SimpleChannel;

public class NetworkHandler {
    private static final String PROTOCOL_VERSION = "1";
    public static final SimpleChannel INSTANCE = NetworkRegistry.newSimpleChannel(
            new ResourceLocation(CollaboMod.MOD_ID, "main"),
            () -> PROTOCOL_VERSION,
            PROTOCOL_VERSION::equals,
            PROTOCOL_VERSION::equals
    );

    public static void register() {
        int id = 0;
        INSTANCE.registerMessage(id++,
                PacketSyncMagicStats.class,
                PacketSyncMagicStats::encode,
                PacketSyncMagicStats::new,
                PacketSyncMagicStats::handle);
//        INSTANCE.registerMessage(id++,
//                PacketMaterialBurst.class,
//                PacketMaterialBurst::toBytes,
//                PacketMaterialBurst::new,
//                PacketMaterialBurst::handle);
        INSTANCE.registerMessage(id++,
                PacketEditCAD.class,
                PacketEditCAD::toBytes,
                PacketEditCAD::new,
                PacketEditCAD::handle);

        INSTANCE.registerMessage(id++,
                PacketSyncCardinalData.class,
                PacketSyncCardinalData::toBytes,
                PacketSyncCardinalData::new,
                PacketSyncCardinalData::handle);
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.network;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.block.entity.MagicConsoleBlockEntity;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.gui.MagicConsoleMenu;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.item.ICAD;
//import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.MagicComponentType;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.SpellResolver;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.VisualMetadata;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.ListTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.StringTag;
import net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraftforge.items.CapabilityItemHandler;
import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkEvent;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.function.Supplier;

public class PacketEditCAD {
    private final List<String> scriptLines;

    public PacketEditCAD(List<String> scriptLines) {
        this.scriptLines = scriptLines;
    }

    public PacketEditCAD(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        this.scriptLines = new ArrayList<>();
        int size = buf.readInt();
        for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
            this.scriptLines.add(buf.readUtf());
        }
    }


    public void toBytes(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        buf.writeInt(scriptLines.size());
        for (String s : scriptLines) {
            buf.writeUtf(s);
        }
    }

    public static void handle(PacketEditCAD msg, Supplier<NetworkEvent.Context> ctx) {
        ctx.get().enqueueWork(() -> {
            ServerPlayer player = ctx.get().getSender();
            if (player == null) return;

            ItemStack targetStack = ItemStack.EMPTY;

            // 笆 蛻・ｲ仙・逅・ 繧ｳ繝ｳ繧ｽ繝ｼ繝ｫ繧帝幕縺・※縺・ｋ縺具ｼ・
            if (player.containerMenu instanceof MagicConsoleMenu menu) {
                // 繧ｳ繝ｳ繧ｽ繝ｼ繝ｫ縺ｮ繧ｹ繝ｭ繝・ヨ0 (CAD繧ｹ繝ｭ繝・ヨ) 繧貞ｯｾ雎｡縺ｫ縺吶ｋ
                targetStack = menu.getSlot(0).getItem();
            } else {
                // 繧ｳ繝ｳ繧ｽ繝ｼ繝ｫ繧帝幕縺・※縺・↑縺・↑繧峨√Γ繧､繝ｳ繝上Φ繝峨ｒ蟇ｾ雎｡縺ｫ縺吶ｋ
                targetStack = player.getItemInHand(InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
            }

            // 繧｢繧､繝・Β縺悟ｭ伜惠縺吶ｋ蝣ｴ蜷医・縺ｿ譖ｸ縺崎ｾｼ縺ｿ
            if (!targetStack.isEmpty()) {
                CompoundTag tag = targetStack.getOrCreateTag();
                ListTag listTag = new ListTag();
                for (String s : msg.scriptLines) {
                    listTag.add(StringTag.valueOf(s));
                }
                tag.put("Script", listTag);
                targetStack.setTag(tag);

                // 繧ｳ繝ｳ繧ｽ繝ｼ繝ｫ縺ｮ蝣ｴ蜷医・繧ｹ繝ｭ繝・ヨ縺ｮ螟画峩騾夂衍縺悟ｿ・ｦ・
                if (player.containerMenu instanceof MagicConsoleMenu) {
                    player.containerMenu.broadcastChanges();
                }
            }
        });
        ctx.get().setPacketHandled(true);
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.network;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.capability.MagicStatsProvider;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.entity.EntitySciencePhenomenon;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.science.ScienceContext;
import net.minecraft.Util;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.TextComponent;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Explosion;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkEvent;

import java.util.function.Supplier;

public class PacketMaterialBurst {
    private final BlockPos targetPos;

    public PacketMaterialBurst(BlockPos pos) {
        this.targetPos = pos;
    }

    public PacketMaterialBurst(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        this.targetPos = buf.readBlockPos();
    }

    public void toBytes(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        buf.writeBlockPos(this.targetPos);
    }

    public void handle(Supplier<NetworkEvent.Context> ctx) {
        ctx.get().enqueueWork(() -> {
            ServerPlayer player = ctx.get().getSender();
            if (player == null) return;
            ServerLevel level = player.getLevel();

            player.getCapability(MagicStatsProvider.PLAYER_MAGIC_STATS).ifPresent(stats -> {
                int cost = 50;

                if (stats.getCurrentPsion() >= cost) {
                    stats.setCurrentPsion(stats.getCurrentPsion() - cost);
                    stats.addMentalLoad(5);

                    player.sendMessage(new TextComponent("ﾂｧc繝槭ユ繝ｪ繧｢繝ｫ繝ｻ繝舌・繧ｹ繝・ 雉ｪ驥上お繝阪Ν繧ｮ繝ｼ螟画鋤繝励Ο繧ｻ繧ｹ螳溯｡・.."), Util.NIL_UUID);

                    ScienceContext burstCtx = ScienceContext.createStrategicClass();

                    // 笆 菫ｮ豁｣: 辷・匱縺ｧ縺ｯ縺ｪ縺上∫ｴ螢翫お繝阪Ν繧ｮ繝ｼ菴薙ｒ險ｭ鄂ｮ縺吶ｋ
//                    EntitySciencePhenomenon burst = new EntitySciencePhenomenon(
//                            level,
//                            Vec3.atCenterOf(targetPos), // BlockPos -> Vec3
//                            burstCtx,
//                            player
//                    );

                    // 髻ｳ: 螟画鋤髢句ｧ九・髻ｳ・磯峭・・
                    level.playSound(null, targetPos, SoundEvents.LIGHTNING_BOLT_THUNDER, SoundSource.WEATHER, 100.0F, 0.5F);

                } else {
                    player.sendMessage(new TextComponent("諠ｳ蟄蝉ｸ崎ｶｳ"), Util.NIL_UUID);
                }
            });
        });
        ctx.get().setPacketHandled(true);
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.network;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.client.ClientCardinalSystem;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.PacketListener;
import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkEvent;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.function.Supplier;

public class PacketSyncCardinalData {
    // 騾∽ｿ｡縺吶ｋ繝・・繧ｿ: 繝√Ε繝ｳ繧ｯ蜀・・繝ｭ繝ｼ繧ｫ繝ｫ蠎ｧ讓・Long) -> 貂ｩ蠎ｦ(Float)
    private final Map<Long, Float> updateMap;
    private final long chunkKey;

    public PacketSyncCardinalData(long chunkKey, Map<Long, Float> map) {
        this.chunkKey = chunkKey;
        this.updateMap = map;
    }

    public PacketSyncCardinalData(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        this.chunkKey = buf.readLong();
        int size = buf.readInt();
        this.updateMap = new HashMap<>(size);
        for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
            this.updateMap.put(buf.readLong(), buf.readFloat());
        }
    }

    public void toBytes(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        buf.writeLong(chunkKey);
        buf.writeInt(updateMap.size());
        updateMap.forEach((pos, temp) -> {
            buf.writeLong(pos);
            buf.writeFloat(temp);
        });
    }

    public void handle(Supplier<NetworkEvent.Context> ctx) {
        ctx.get().enqueueWork(() -> {
            // 繧ｯ繝ｩ繧､繧｢繝ｳ繝亥・縺ｮ繧ｷ繧ｹ繝・Β縺ｫ繝・・繧ｿ繧呈ｸ｡縺・
            ClientCardinalSystem.receiveUpdate(chunkKey, updateMap);
        });
        ctx.get().setPacketHandled(true);
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.network;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.capability.MagicStatsProvider;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.client.ClientPacketHandler;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkEvent;

import java.util.function.Supplier;
public class PacketSyncMagicStats {
    private final int currentPsion;
    private final int maxPsion;
    private final int calculationArea;
    private final int mentalLoad;

    // 繧ｳ繝ｳ繧ｹ繝医Λ繧ｯ繧ｿ・壹ョ繝ｼ繧ｿ繧偵そ繝・ヨ縺吶ｋ
    public PacketSyncMagicStats(int currentPsion, int maxPsion, int calculationArea, int mentalLoad) {
        this.currentPsion = currentPsion;
        this.maxPsion = maxPsion;
        this.calculationArea = calculationArea;
        this.mentalLoad = mentalLoad;
    }

    // 繝・さ繝ｼ繝会ｼ壹ヰ繧､繝亥・縺九ｉ繝・・繧ｿ繧貞叙繧雁・縺呻ｼ亥女菫｡譎ゑｼ・
    public PacketSyncMagicStats(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        this.currentPsion = buf.readInt();
        this.maxPsion = buf.readInt();
        this.calculationArea = buf.readInt();
        this.mentalLoad = buf.readInt();
    }

    // 繧ｨ繝ｳ繧ｳ繝ｼ繝会ｼ壹ョ繝ｼ繧ｿ繧偵ヰ繧､繝亥・縺ｫ螟画鋤縺吶ｋ・磯∽ｿ｡譎ゑｼ・
    public void encode(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        buf.writeInt(currentPsion);
        buf.writeInt(maxPsion);
        buf.writeInt(calculationArea);
        buf.writeInt(mentalLoad);
    }

    // 繝上Φ繝峨Ν・壹ョ繝ｼ繧ｿ繧貞女縺大叙縺｣縺溷ｾ後・蜃ｦ逅・ｼ医け繝ｩ繧､繧｢繝ｳ繝亥・縺ｧ螳溯｡鯉ｼ・

    public void handle(Supplier<NetworkEvent.Context> ctx) {
        ctx.get().enqueueWork(() -> {
            // 繧ｯ繝ｩ繧､繧｢繝ｳ繝亥・縺ｧ蜿嶺ｿ｡縺励◆蝣ｴ蜷医・縺ｿ蜃ｦ逅・
            if (ctx.get().getDirection().getReceptionSide().isClient()) {
                // 蟆ら畑繝上Φ繝峨Λ繝ｼ縺ｫ荳ｸ謚輔￡縺吶ｋ・医％繧後〒繧ｵ繝ｼ繝舌・蛛ｴ縺ｧ縺ｮ繧ｯ繝ｩ繝・す繝･繧貞屓驕ｿ・・
                ClientPacketHandler.handlePacket(this.currentPsion, this.maxPsion, this.calculationArea, this.mentalLoad);
            }
        });
        ctx.get().setPacketHandled(true);
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.physics;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.SpellContext;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.physics.processor.KineticProcessor;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.physics.processor.StructuralProcessor;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.physics.processor.ThermalProcessor;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.physics.processor.WaveProcessor;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.science.ScienceContext;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

public class PhysicsSystem {
    private static final KineticProcessor kinetic = new KineticProcessor();
    private static final ThermalProcessor thermal = new ThermalProcessor();
    private static final StructuralProcessor structural = new StructuralProcessor();
    private static final WaveProcessor wave = new WaveProcessor();

    /**
     * 遘大ｭｦ繝代Λ繝｡繝ｼ繧ｿ(ctx)縺ｫ蝓ｺ縺･縺・※縲∫黄逅・樟雎｡繧剃ｸ也阜縺ｫ驕ｩ逕ｨ縺吶ｋ
     */
    public static void applyPhysics(Level level, Vec3 origin, ScienceContext ctx, LivingEntity caster, LivingEntity target) {
        if (level.isClientSide) return;

        BlockPos center = new BlockPos(origin);

        // 1. 辭ｱ蜉帛ｭｦ (貂ｩ蠎ｦ)
        if (Math.abs(ctx.temperature - 300.0F) > 50.0F) {
            // 笆 菫ｮ豁｣: caster 繧呈ｸ｡縺・
            thermal.process(level, center, ctx, caster);
        }

        // 2. 讒矩蜉帛ｭｦ (蛻・ｧ｣)
        if (ctx.compMatter > 0.0F) {
            structural.process(level, center, ctx);
        }

        // 3. 驕句虚蜉帛ｭｦ (遘ｻ蜍輔・陦晄茶繝ｻ蜉驥・
        if (ctx.velocity > 1.0F || ctx.mass > 0.0F || ctx.energy > 10.0F) {
            kinetic.process(level, center, ctx, caster, target);
        }

        // 4. 豕｢蜍・(髦ｲ蠕｡繝ｻ蟷ｲ貂・
        if (ctx.compWave > 0.0F || ctx.compLight > 0.0F || ctx.compShield > 0.0F) {
            wave.process(level, center, ctx, caster, target);
        }
    }
}

==================================================
FILE_PATH: C:\mincraft-mod\CollaboMod\src\main\java\com\COLLABOMOD\collabomod\physics\processor\KineticProcessor.java
==================================================
package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.physics.processor;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.SpellContext;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.science.ScienceContext;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffects;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

import java.util.List;

public class KineticProcessor {

    public void process(Level level, BlockPos center, ScienceContext ctx, LivingEntity caster, LivingEntity target) {
        float radius = ctx.radius;
        Vec3 origin = Vec3.atCenterOf(center);
        AABB area = new AABB(center).inflate(radius);
        List<Entity> entities = level.getEntities(null, area);

        // 1. 蜉騾・(Accel)
        if (ctx.velocity > 1.5F) {
            for (Entity e : entities) {
                if (target != null && e != target) continue;
                Vec3 accel = e.position().subtract(origin).normalize().scale(ctx.velocity * 0.5);
                e.setDeltaMovement(e.getDeltaMovement().add(accel));
                e.hurtMarked = true;
                e.fallDistance = 0;
            }
        }

        // 2. 蜉驥・(Weight)
        if (ctx.mass > 0.0F) {
            for (Entity e : entities) {
                if (e == caster) continue;
                if (e instanceof LivingEntity living) {
                    double gravity = ctx.mass * 0.05;
                    e.setDeltaMovement(e.getDeltaMovement().add(0, -gravity, 0));
                    int amplifier = (int)(ctx.mass / 20.0F);
                    living.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.MOVEMENT_SLOWDOWN, 60, amplifier));
                    living.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.JUMP, 60, 128));
                }
            }
        }

        // 3. 陦晄茶豕｢ (Shockwave)
        if (ctx.energy > 50.0F) {
            for (Entity e : entities) {
                if (e instanceof LivingEntity && e != caster) {
                    double dist = e.distanceToSqr(origin);
                    if (dist < radius * radius) {
                        Vec3 dir = e.position().subtract(origin).normalize();
                        double power = (ctx.energy * 0.05) * (1.0 - (Math.sqrt(dist) / radius));
                        e.setDeltaMovement(e.getDeltaMovement().add(dir.scale(power)));
                        e.hurtMarked = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            // 繝悶Ο繝・け遐ｴ螢・(縺薙ｌ繧よｮｻ縺ｮ縺ｿ縺ｫ縺吶ｋ)
            if (ctx.energy > 200.0F) {
                int r = (int) Math.ceil(radius);
                float innerRadius = Math.max(0, radius - 1.5F);
                float innerSq = innerRadius * innerRadius;
                float outerSq = radius * radius;

                for (int x = -r; x <= r; x++) {
                    for (int y = -r; y <= r; y++) {
                        for (int z = -r; z <= r; z++) {
                            double distSq = x*x + y*y + z*z;
                            if (distSq > outerSq || distSq < innerSq) continue; // 笆 譛驕ｩ蛹・

                            BlockPos pos = center.offset(x, y, z);
                            BlockState state = level.getBlockState(pos);
                            float hardness = state.getDestroySpeed(level, pos);
                            if (hardness >= 0.0F && hardness <= 0.3F) {
                                level.destroyBlock(pos, true);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE_PATH: C:\mincraft-mod\CollaboMod\src\main\java\com\COLLABOMOD\collabomod\physics\processor\StructuralProcessor.java
==================================================
package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.physics.processor;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.science.ScienceContext;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;

public class StructuralProcessor {

    public void process(Level level, BlockPos center, ScienceContext ctx) {
        float decompositionPower = ctx.compMatter * ctx.energy;
        float radius = ctx.radius;
        // 蜃ｦ逅・ｯ・峇縺ｮ譛驕ｩ蛹・ 蜊雁ｾ・′螟ｧ縺阪☆縺弱ｋ蝣ｴ蜷医・蜃ｦ逅・ｒ髢灘ｼ輔￥縺ｪ縺ｩ縺ｮ蟇ｾ遲悶ｂ蜿ｯ閭ｽ縺ｧ縺吶′縲・
        // 縺ｾ縺壹・縲梧ｮｻ・・hell・峨阪・縺ｿ繧貞・逅・☆繧九ｈ縺・↓縺励∪縺吶・

        int range = (int) Math.ceil(radius);
        float innerRadius = Math.max(0, radius - 2.0F); // 谿ｻ縺ｮ蜴壹∩2繝悶Ο繝・け蛻・
        float innerSq = innerRadius * innerRadius;
        float outerSq = radius * radius;

        for (int x = -range; x <= range; x++) {
            for (int y = -range; y <= range; y++) {
                for (int z = -range; z <= range; z++) {
                    double distSq = x*x + y*y + z*z;

                    // 笆 譛驕ｩ蛹・ 縲檎ｯ・峇螟悶阪∪縺溘・縲悟・逅・ｸ医∩縺ｮ蜀・・縲阪↑繧峨せ繧ｭ繝・・
                    if (distSq > outerSq || distSq < innerSq) continue;

                    BlockPos pos = center.offset(x, y, z);
                    BlockState state = level.getBlockState(pos);
                    if (state.isAir()) continue;

                    float hardness = state.getDestroySpeed(level, pos);
                    if (hardness < 0) continue;

                    if (decompositionPower > hardness * 5.0F || decompositionPower > 1000.0F) {
                        level.removeBlock(pos, false);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

==================================================
FILE_PATH: C:\mincraft-mod\CollaboMod\src\main\java\com\COLLABOMOD\collabomod\physics\processor\ThermalProcessor.java
==================================================
package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.physics.processor;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.science.ScienceContext;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.world.cardinal.WorldCardinalSystem;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;

public class ThermalProcessor {
    public void process(Level level, BlockPos center, ScienceContext ctx, LivingEntity caster) {
        float temp = ctx.temperature;
        float radius = ctx.radius;

        int range = (int) Math.ceil(radius);
        float innerRadius = Math.max(0, radius - 2.0F);
        float innerSq = innerRadius * innerRadius;
        float outerSq = radius * radius;

        WorldCardinalSystem cardinal = null;
        if (level instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel) {
            cardinal = WorldCardinalSystem.get(serverLevel);
        }

        for (int x = -range; x <= range; x++) {
            for (int y = -range; y <= range; y++) {
                for (int z = -range; z <= range; z++) {
                    double distSq = x*x + y*y + z*z;
                    if (distSq > outerSq || distSq < innerSq) continue;

                    BlockPos pos = center.offset(x, y, z);

                    if (cardinal != null && caster != null) {
                        float diff = temp - 300.0F;
                        cardinal.addTemperature(level, pos, diff);
                    }

                    // 笆笆笆 蜊ｳ譎ら黄逅・柑譫・笆笆笆
                    BlockState state = level.getBlockState(pos);

                    // 1000蠎ｦ雜・∴
                    if (temp > 1000.0F) {
                        // 闕峨↑縺ｩ繧堤┥蜊ｴ
                        if (state.is(Blocks.GRASS) || state.is(Blocks.TALL_GRASS) || state.is(Blocks.FERN)) {
                            level.setBlock(pos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), 3);
                        }
                        // 笘・ｿｮ豁｣: 豌ｴ縺ｮ蜊ｳ譎り頂逋ｺ
                        else if (state.is(Blocks.WATER)) {
                            level.setBlock(pos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), 3);
                            if (level instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel) {
                                serverLevel.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.CLOUD, pos.getX()+0.5, pos.getY()+0.5, pos.getZ()+0.5, 1, 0.2, 0.2, 0.2, 0.05);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE_PATH: C:\mincraft-mod\CollaboMod\src\main\java\com\COLLABOMOD\collabomod\physics\processor\WaveProcessor.java
==================================================
package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.physics.processor;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.SpellContext;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.science.ScienceContext;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.entity.EntityMagicSequence;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffects;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.Projectile;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

import java.util.List;

public class WaveProcessor {
    public void process(Level level, BlockPos center, ScienceContext ctx, LivingEntity caster, LivingEntity target) {
//        Level level = ctx.level;
//        Vec3 origin = ctx.origin;
//        ScienceContext science = ctx.science;
        float radius = ctx.radius;
        Vec3 origin = Vec3.atCenterOf(center);
        AABB area = new AABB(new BlockPos(center)).inflate(radius);

        // 笆 1. 髦ｲ蠕｡繝ｻ蟷ｲ貂・(Defense / Jamming)
        // 髦ｲ蠕｡謌仙・(compShield)縺後≠繧句ｴ蜷・
        if (ctx.compShield > 0.0F) {
            List<Entity> entities = level.getEntities(null, area);
            for (Entity e : entities) {
                if (e instanceof EntityMagicSequence) e.discard();
                if (e instanceof Projectile) e.discard();

                if (e instanceof LivingEntity living) {
                    // 笆 陦楢・∪縺溘・繧ｿ繝ｼ繧ｲ繝・ヨ・亥袖譁ｹ・峨↑繧蛾亟蠕｡繝舌ヵ
                    if (e == caster || e == target) {
                        int duration = (int)(ctx.energy * 2);
                        if (duration < 60) duration = 60;
                        living.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.DAMAGE_RESISTANCE, duration, 1));
                        if (ctx.temperature < 200.0F) {
                            living.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.FIRE_RESISTANCE, duration, 0));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 笆 2. 騾城℃繝ｻ蜈ｱ魑ｴ (Resonance) - 謾ｻ謦・噪豕｢蜍・
        if (ctx.compWave > 0.8F) {
            List<Entity> targets = level.getEntities(null, area);
            for (Entity e : targets) {
                // 笆 陦楢・・繝繝｡繝ｼ繧ｸ繧貞女縺代↑縺・
                if (e instanceof LivingEntity && e != caster) {
                    if (e.distanceToSqr(center.getX(), center.getY(), center.getZ()) > radius * radius) continue;
                    float damage = ctx.energy * 0.1F;
                    e.hurt(new DamageSource("magic").bypassArmor().setMagic(), damage);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.register;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.main.CollaboMod;
import net.minecraft.world.item.BlockItem;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Block;
import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.IEventBus;
import net.minecraftforge.registries.DeferredRegister;
import net.minecraftforge.registries.ForgeRegistries;
import net.minecraftforge.registries.RegistryObject;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.block.MagicConsoleBlock;

import java.util.function.Supplier;

public class BlockRegister {
    public static final DeferredRegister<Block> BLOCKS = DeferredRegister.create(ForgeRegistries.BLOCKS, CollaboMod.MOD_ID);
    public static final RegistryObject<Block> MAGIC_CONSOLE = registerBlockItem("magic_console", MagicConsoleBlock::new);





    // 繝悶Ο繝・け繧｢繧､繝・Β菴懈・逕ｨ繝｡繧ｽ繝・ラ 蝓ｺ譛ｬ逧・↓隗ｦ繧峨↑縺・
    private static <T extends Block> RegistryObject<T> registerBlockItem(String name, Supplier<T> supplier) {
        // 繝ｬ繧ｸ繧ｹ繝医Μ縺ｫ繝悶Ο繝・け繧定ｿｽ蜉
        RegistryObject<T> block = BLOCKS.register(name, supplier);
        // 繝悶Ο繝・け繧｢繧､繝・Β繧偵い繧､繝・Β繝ｬ繧ｸ繧ｹ繝医Μ縺ｫ霑ｽ蜉
        ItemRegister.ITEMS.register(name,
                () -> new BlockItem(block.get(), new Item.Properties().tab(CollaboMod.COLLABOMOD_BLOCK_TAB)));
        return block;
    }

    public static void register(IEventBus eventBus) {
        // 繝ｬ繧ｸ繧ｹ繝医Μ繧偵う繝吶Φ繝医ヰ繧ｹ縺ｫ逋ｻ骭ｲ
        BLOCKS.register(eventBus);
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.science;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.EnumMagicShape;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.VisualMetadata;
import com.mojang.math.Vector3f;

public class ScienceContext {

    // 蝓ｺ譛ｬ迚ｩ逅・㍼
    public float energy = 10.0F;
    public float temperature = 300.0F;
    public float mass = 0.0F;
    public float density = 1.0F;

    // 謖吝虚
    public float velocity = 1.0F;
    public float radius = 1.0F;
    public PhenomenonType type = PhenomenonType.POINT;

    // 謌仙・ (0.0~1.0)
    public float compMatter = 0.0F; // 迚ｩ雉ｪ蛻・ｧ｣
    public float compWave = 0.0F;   // 謖ｯ蜍・
    public float compLight = 0.0F;  // 蜈・
    public float compShield = 0.0F; // 笆 霑ｽ蜉: 髦ｲ蠕｡繝ｻ蟷ｲ貂画・蛻・

    // 蜃ｺ蜉帙＆繧後ｋ隕九◆逶ｮ
    public VisualMetadata visuals = new VisualMetadata();

    // 險育ｮ礼畑荳譎ょ､画焚
    public Vector3f coreColor = new Vector3f(1,1,1);
    public Vector3f outerColor = new Vector3f(1,1,1);
    public float lightningIntensity = 0.0F;
    public float distortionIntensity = 0.0F;

    public ScienceContext() {}

    public static ScienceContext createStrategicClass() {
        ScienceContext ctx = new ScienceContext();
        ctx.type = PhenomenonType.SPHERE_EXPANSION;
        ctx.energy = 10000.0F;
        ctx.radius = 50.0F;
        ctx.velocity = 0.2F;
        ctx.compMatter = 1.0F;
        ctx.temperature = 5000.0F;
        // VisualMetadata繧ゅ％縺薙〒險ｭ螳壹＠縺ｦ縺翫￥縺ｨ螳牙・
        ctx.visuals = new VisualMetadata("material_burst", 100, new Vector3f(0.2F, 0.9F, 1.0F), 2.0F);
        ctx.visuals = new VisualMetadata("material_burst", 100, new Vector3f(0.2F, 0.9F, 1.0F), 2.0F);
        ctx.visuals.shape = EnumMagicShape.SPHERE;
        ctx.visuals.hasLightning = true;

        return ctx;
    }
}

//繝ｩ繝ｳ繝繝逕滓・
// 莠呈鋤諤ｧ逕ｨ (Seed縺ｪ縺励〒蜻ｼ縺ｰ繧後◆繧峨Λ繝ｳ繝繝)
public static void simulateVisuals(ScienceContext ctx) {
    simulateVisuals(ctx, new java.util.Random().nextInt());
}

    // 笆 譛ｬ蜻ｽ: Seed縺ｫ蝓ｺ縺･縺乗緒逕ｻ逕滓・
    public static void simulateVisuals(ScienceContext ctx, int seed) {

        // 1. AI (AnalysisEngine) 縺ｫ繝吶・繧ｹ縺ｨ縺ｪ繧区緒逕ｻ險ｭ螳壹ｒ逕滓・縺輔○繧・
        // 縺薙％縺ｧ Shape, Color, Spiky, Wavy 縺ｪ縺ｩ縺・Seed 縺ｫ蝓ｺ縺･縺・※豎ｺ螳壹＆繧後ｋ
        // 螻樊ｧ繝吶け繝医Ν縺ｯ繝繝溘・ (new float[6]) 縺ｧ貂｡縺吶′縲∝ｰ・擂逧・↓縺ｯ ctx.temperature 遲峨°繧我ｽ懈・縺励※繧り憶縺・
        VisualMetadata aiMeta = AnalysisEngine.deriveVisualsFromAttributes(new float[6], seed);

        // 逕滓・縺輔ｌ縺溘Γ繧ｿ繝・・繧ｿ繧・Context 縺ｫ驕ｩ逕ｨ
        ctx.visuals = aiMeta;
        VisualMetadata v = ctx.visuals;

        // 2. 迚ｩ逅・㍼縺ｫ繧医ｋ縲後せ繧ｱ繝ｼ繝ｪ繝ｳ繧ｰ縲阪・縺ｿ驕ｩ逕ｨ (蠖｢迥ｶ繧・牡縺ｯAI縺梧ｱｺ繧√◆繧ゅ・繧貞ｰ企㍾)

        // 蠑ｷ蠎ｦ險育ｮ・
        float energyFactor = Math.min(1.0F, ctx.energy / 10000.0F);
        float tempFactor = Math.min(1.0F, (ctx.temperature - 300.0F) / 4700.0F);
        v.normalizedIntensity = Math.max(energyFactor, tempFactor);

        // 繧ｵ繧､繧ｺ險育ｮ・(迚ｩ逅・噪縺ｪ蜊雁ｾ・↓蜷医ｏ縺帙ｋ)
        if (ctx.type == PhenomenonType.SPHERE_EXPANSION) {
            v.scale = ctx.radius; // 蜊雁ｾ・ｒ驕ｩ逕ｨ
            // 窶ｻ縺薙％縺ｧ v.shape = SPHERE 縺ｨ譖ｸ縺・※縺・◆陦後ｒ蜑企勁・・
            // AI縺・CYLINDER 繧帝∈繧薙□縺ｪ繧峨，YLINDER 縺ｮ縺ｾ縺ｾ蜊雁ｾ・□縺鷹←逕ｨ縺輔ｌ繧九・
        }
        else {
            v.scale = 1.0F + (ctx.energy / 100.0F);
        }

        // 3. 迚ｩ逅・噪縺ｪ蠕ｮ隱ｿ謨ｴ (陬懈ｭ｣)
        // AI縺梧ｱｺ螳壹＠縺溯牡縺ｫ蟇ｾ縺励※縲∵･ｵ遶ｯ縺ｪ貂ｩ蠎ｦ縺ｪ繧牙ｰ代＠縺縺大刈邂励☆繧狗ｨ句ｺｦ縺ｫ逡吶ａ繧・
        if (ctx.temperature > 2000.0F) {
            // 逋ｽ辭ｱ (蟆代＠逋ｽ繧呈ｷｷ縺懊ｋ)
            v.mainColor.add(0.2f, 0.2f, 0.2f);
        }

        // 繧ｷ繝ｼ繝ｫ繝画・蛻・′縺ゅｋ蝣ｴ蜷医・乗・蠎ｦ繧・ｳｪ諢溘ｒ縺・§繧・(蠖｢迥ｶ縺ｯ螟峨∴縺ｪ縺・
        if (ctx.compShield > 0.0F) {
            v.isSolid = true;
        }
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.util;
import com.mojang.math.Vector3f;
public enum MagicSpellType {
    // 蜷榊燕(繧ｭ繝｣繧ｹ繝域凾髢・ 濶ｲ, 謇句・霑ｽ蠕薙☆繧九°)
    GRAM_DEMOLITION(10, new Vector3f(0.2F, 0.9F, 1.0F), true),

    // 笆 霑ｽ蜉: 繧ｨ繧｢繝ｻ繝舌Ξ繝・ヨ (0.75遘・ 逋ｽ, 險ｭ鄂ｮ蝙・false)
    AIR_BULLET(30, new Vector3f(0.9F, 0.9F, 1.0F), false),

    MIST_DISPERSION(20, new Vector3f(1.0F, 0.2F, 0.2F), false),
    REGROWTH(40, new Vector3f(0.2F, 1.0F, 0.5F), false),
    MATERIAL_BURST(100, new Vector3f(1.0F, 0.6F, 0.0F), false);

    public final int castTime;
    public final Vector3f color;
    public final boolean isAttachedToCaster;

    MagicSpellType(int castTime, Vector3f color, boolean isAttachedToCaster) {
        this.castTime = castTime;
        this.color = color;
        this.isAttachedToCaster = isAttachedToCaster;
    }
}

FILE_PATH: C:\mincraft-mod\CollaboMod\src\main\java\com\COLLABOMOD\collabomod\util\PsionParticleUtil.java

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.util;

import com.mojang.math.Vector3f; // 繧ゅ＠繧ｨ繝ｩ繝ｼ縺悟・繧句ｴ蜷医・ net.minecraft.core.Vector3f 縺・com.mojang.math.Vector3f
import net.minecraft.core.particles.DustParticleOptions;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleOptions;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

public class PsionParticleUtil {
    // 繧ｵ繧､繧ｪ繝ｳ縺ｮ蝓ｺ譛ｬ濶ｲ・医す繧｢繝ｳ繝悶Ν繝ｼ・・
    // DustParticleOptions 縺ｯ RGB (0.0 ~ 1.0) 縺ｧ謖・ｮ壹＠縺ｾ縺・
    // R:0.2, G:0.9, B:1.0 -> 譏弱ｋ縺・ｰｴ濶ｲ
    private static final Vector3f PSION_COLOR = new Vector3f(0.2F, 0.9F, 1.0F);

    /**
     * 謖・ｮ壹＠縺滉ｽ咲ｽｮ縺九ｉ縲∬ｦ也ｷ壽婿蜷代↓蝙ら峩縺ｪ繝ｪ繝ｳ繧ｰ・域ｳ｢蜍包ｼ峨ｒ謠冗判縺吶ｋ
     * @param level 繝ｯ繝ｼ繝ｫ繝・
     * @param center 荳ｭ蠢・ｺｧ讓呻ｼ磯潟蜿｣縺ｮ菴咲ｽｮ・・
     * @param direction 鬟帙・縺呎婿蜷托ｼ郁ｦ也ｷ壹・繧ｯ繝医Ν・・
     * @param radius 蜊雁ｾ・
     * @param density 邊貞ｭ舌・蟇・ｺｦ・亥・繧剃ｽ募・蜑ｲ縺吶ｋ縺具ｼ・
     */
    public static void spawnPsionRing(Level level, Vec3 center, Vec3 direction, float radius, int density) {
        // 譁ｹ蜷代・繧ｯ繝医Ν繧呈ｭ｣隕丞喧
        Vec3 view = direction.normalize();

        // 笆 蜀・ｒ謠上￥縺溘ａ縺ｮ蝓ｺ貅冶ｻｸ・亥承繝吶け繝医Ν縺ｨ荳翫・繧ｯ繝医Ν・峨ｒ險育ｮ励☆繧・
        // 1. 縲悟承繝吶け繝医Ν縲・= 縲瑚ｦ也ｷ壹阪→縲檎悄荳・0,1,0)縲阪・螟也ｩ・
        Vec3 right = view.cross(new Vec3(0, 1, 0)).normalize();

        // 窶ｻ逵滉ｸ翫ｄ逵滉ｸ九ｒ蜷代＞縺ｦ縺・ｋ縺ｨ螟也ｩ阪′0縺ｫ縺ｪ繧九◆繧∝ｯｾ遲・
        if (right.lengthSqr() < 1.0E-4D) {
            // 隕也ｷ壹′Y霆ｸ荳ｦ陦後↑繧峨々霆ｸ繧貞承縺ｨ縺吶ｋ
            right = new Vec3(1, 0, 0);
        }

        // 2. 縲御ｸ翫・繧ｯ繝医Ν縲・= 縲悟承縲阪→縲瑚ｦ也ｷ壹阪・螟也ｩ搾ｼ医％繧後〒繧ｫ繝｡繝ｩ縺ｫ蟇ｾ縺励※蝙ら峩縺ｪ髱｢縺後〒縺阪ｋ・・
        Vec3 up = right.cross(view).normalize();

        // 笆 繝代・繝・ぅ繧ｯ繝ｫ縺ｮ險ｭ螳・
        // scale: 1.0F (螟ｧ縺阪＆)
        ParticleOptions particle = new DustParticleOptions(PSION_COLOR, 1.0F);

        // 笆 蜀・捉荳翫↓繝代・繝・ぅ繧ｯ繝ｫ繧帝・鄂ｮ
        for (int i = 0; i < density; i++) {
            // 隗貞ｺｦ (0 ・・2ﾏ)
            double angle = 2 * Math.PI * i / density;

            // 蜀・捉荳翫・轤ｹ縺ｮ險育ｮ・
            // P = Center + (Right * cosﾎｸ * r) + (Up * sinﾎｸ * r)
            double xOffset = (right.x * Math.cos(angle) + up.x * Math.sin(angle)) * radius;
            double yOffset = (right.y * Math.cos(angle) + up.y * Math.sin(angle)) * radius;
            double zOffset = (right.z * Math.cos(angle) + up.z * Math.sin(angle)) * radius;

            level.addParticle(particle,
                    center.x + xOffset,
                    center.y + yOffset,
                    center.z + zOffset,
                    0, 0, 0); // 騾溷ｺｦ縺ｯ0
        }
    }
}

==================================================
package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.world.cardinal;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.EnumMagicAnchor;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.VisualMetadata;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

import java.util.Random;

public class CardinalSpaceManager {

    private static final Random random = new Random();

    /**
     * 繧ｫ繝ｼ繝・ぅ繝翫Ν繝ｻ繧ｷ繧ｹ繝・Β縺ｫ繧医ｋ譛驕ｩ蠎ｧ讓吶・邂怜・
     */
    public static Vec3 findOptimalPosition(LivingEntity caster, LivingEntity target, VisualMetadata meta) {
        EnumMagicAnchor anchor = meta.anchorType;
        Vec3 origin = caster.position();

        // 笆 蝓ｺ貅悶・繧ｯ繝医Ν縺ｮ險育ｮ・
        // 隕也ｷ・LookAngle)縺ｧ縺ｯ縺ｪ縺上∽ｽ薙・蜷代″(Forward)繧剃ｽｿ縺・％縺ｨ縺ｧ縲∬ｦ也せ遘ｻ蜍輔↓繧医ｋ繝悶Ξ繧偵↑縺上☆
        // Y霆ｸ謌仙・繧偵き繝・ヨ縺励※豌ｴ蟷ｳ蛹・
        Vec3 look = caster.getLookAngle();
        Vec3 forward = new Vec3(look.x, 0, look.z).normalize();

        // 蜿ｳ繝吶け繝医Ν (Y霆ｸ縺ｨ縺ｮ螟也ｩ・
        Vec3 right = forward.cross(new Vec3(0, 1, 0)).normalize();
        Vec3 up = new Vec3(0, 1, 0);

        // 笆 A. 遨ｺ荳ｭ螻暮幕 (RANDOM_AIR) -> 縲蝉ｿｮ豁｣縲大燕譁ｹ遨ｺ髢灘・謨｣繝ｭ繧ｸ繝・け
        if (anchor == EnumMagicAnchor.RANDOM_AIR) {
            // 繧ｷ繝ｼ繝牙､繧探ick縺斐→縺ｫ縺壹ｉ縺励∝酔荳Tick蜀・〒繧ょ他縺ｳ蜃ｺ縺鈴・〒縺ｰ繧峨￠繧九ｈ縺・↓縺吶ｋ
            long seed = System.nanoTime() ^ caster.tickCount;
            Random localRand = new Random(seed);

            // 1. 螂･陦後″ (Depth): 陦楢・°繧・3.0m ~ 7.0m 髮｢縺・
            // 縺薙ｌ縺ｫ繧医ｊ縲檎岼縺ｮ蜑阪阪〒縺ｯ縺ｪ縺上檎ｩｺ髢薙阪↓蜃ｺ迴ｾ縺吶ｋ
            double depth = 6.0 + localRand.nextDouble() * 15.0;

            // 2. 讓ｪ蟷・(Width): 蟾ｦ蜿ｳ縺ｫ ﾂｱ3.0m (險・m)
            // 蠎・＞遽・峇縺ｫ謨｣繧峨☆縺薙→縺ｧ驥阪↑繧翫ｒ髦ｲ豁｢
            double widthOffset = (localRand.nextDouble() - 0.8) * 10.0;

            // 3. 鬮倥＆ (Height): 逶ｮ縺ｮ鬮倥＆ ﾂｱ1.5m
            // 遶倶ｽ鍋噪縺ｫ驟咲ｽｮ
            double heightOffset = (localRand.nextDouble() - 0.8) * 10.0;

            // 蠎ｧ讓吝粋謌・ 蜴溽せ(雜ｳ蜈・ + (蜑・* depth) + (蜿ｳ * width) + (荳・* (eyeHeight + offset))
            double eyeHeight = caster.getEyeHeight();

            return origin
                    .add(forward.scale(depth))
                    .add(right.scale(widthOffset))
                    .add(up.scale(eyeHeight + heightOffset));
        }

        // 笆 B. 繧ｿ繝ｼ繧ｲ繝・ヨ蝗ｺ螳・(雜ｳ蜈・ｨｭ鄂ｮ)
        else if (anchor == EnumMagicAnchor.TARGET_ANCHORED) {
            if (target != null) {
                return target.position().add(0, 0.1, 0);
            } else {
                // 繧ｿ繝ｼ繧ｲ繝・ヨ荳榊惠譎ゅ・縲∝燕譁ｹ荳螳夊ｷ晞屬縺ｮ蝨ｰ髱｢
                return origin.add(forward.scale(5.0)).with(net.minecraft.core.Direction.Axis.Y, origin.y);
            }
        }

        // 笆 C. 陦楢・ｿｽ蠕・(CASTER)
        else if (anchor == EnumMagicAnchor.CASTER_ANCHORED) {
            // 逶ｮ縺ｮ蜑・1.5m (縺薙％縺ｯ隕也ｷ夊ｿｽ蠕薙〒OK)
            return caster.getEyePosition().add(look.scale(2.5));
        }

        // 笆 D. 繝・ヵ繧ｩ繝ｫ繝・(FIXED遲・
        else {
            return origin.add(forward.scale(3.0)).add(0, 1.5, 0);
        }
    }
}

FILE_PATH: C:\mincraft-mod\CollaboMod\src\main\java\com\COLLABOMOD\collabomod\world\cardinal\EnvironmentChunkData.java
==================================================
package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.world.cardinal;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.ListTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.Tag;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.Map;

public class EnvironmentChunkData {

    // 繝ｭ繝ｼ繧ｫ繝ｫ蠎ｧ讓・Long) -> 蛟､
    private final Map<Long, Float> temperatureMap = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<Long, Float> psionDensityMap = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<Long, Integer> magicHashMap = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<Long, Float> entropyMap = new HashMap<>();

    private boolean isDirty = false;

    public EnvironmentChunkData() {
    }

    // 譎る俣邨碁℃蜃ｦ逅・
    public void tick() {
        boolean changed = false;

        // 1. 貂ｩ蠎ｦ縺ｮ貂幄｡ｰ (300K縺ｫ霑代▼縺・
        if (!temperatureMap.isEmpty()) {
            Iterator<Map.Entry<Long, Float>> it = temperatureMap.entrySet().iterator();
            while (it.hasNext()) {
                Map.Entry<Long, Float> entry = it.next();
                float current = entry.getValue();
                float diff = current - 300.0F;

                if (Math.abs(diff) < 1.0F) {
                    it.remove();
                    changed = true;
                } else {
                    float next = current - diff * 0.01F; // 1%縺壹▽蟶ｸ貂ｩ縺ｸ
                    entry.setValue(next);
                    changed = true;
                }
            }
        }

        // 2. 繧ｵ繧､繧ｪ繝ｳ豼・ｺｦ縺ｮ貂幄｡ｰ
        if (!psionDensityMap.isEmpty()) {
            Iterator<Map.Entry<Long, Float>> it = psionDensityMap.entrySet().iterator();
            while (it.hasNext()) {
                Map.Entry<Long, Float> entry = it.next();
                float val = entry.getValue();
                if (val < 0.1F) {
                    it.remove();
                    changed = true;
                } else {
                    entry.setValue(val * 0.99F);
                    changed = true;
                }
            }
        }

        // 3. 繧ｨ繝ｳ繝医Ο繝斐・縺ｮ隗｣豸・
        if (!entropyMap.isEmpty()) {
            Iterator<Map.Entry<Long, Float>> it = entropyMap.entrySet().iterator();
            while (it.hasNext()) {
                Map.Entry<Long, Float> entry = it.next();
                float val = entry.getValue();
                if (val < 0.1F) {
                    it.remove();
                    changed = true;
                } else {
                    entry.setValue(val * 0.98F);
                    changed = true;
                }
            }
        }

        if (changed) {
            markDirty();
        }
    }

    public Map<Long, Float> getAllTemperatures() {
        return temperatureMap;
    }

    public Map<Long, Float> getAllPsionDensities() {
        return psionDensityMap;
    }

    // --- 貂ｩ蠎ｦ謫堺ｽ・---
    public float getTemperature(BlockPos pos) {
        long key = getLocalKey(pos);
        return temperatureMap.getOrDefault(key, 300.0F);
    }

    public void setTemperature(BlockPos pos, float temp) {
        long key = getLocalKey(pos);
        setTemperatureDirect(key, temp);
    }

    // 笆 霑ｽ蜉: 繝代こ繝・ヨ蜿嶺ｿ｡譎ら畑 (Raw Key Setter)
    public void setTemperatureDirect(long key, float temp) {
        if (Math.abs(temp - 300.0F) < 1.0F) {
            if (temperatureMap.remove(key) != null) markDirty();
        } else {
            temperatureMap.put(key, temp);
            markDirty();
        }
    }

    // --- 繧ｵ繧､繧ｪ繝ｳ豼・ｺｦ謫堺ｽ・---
    public float getPsionDensity(BlockPos pos) {
        long key = getLocalKey(pos);
        return psionDensityMap.getOrDefault(key, 0.0F);
    }

    public void setPsionDensity(BlockPos pos, float density) {
        long key = getLocalKey(pos);
        setPsionDensityDirect(key, density);
    }

    // 笆 霑ｽ蜉: 繝代こ繝・ヨ蜿嶺ｿ｡譎ら畑
    public void setPsionDensityDirect(long key, float density) {
        if (density <= 0.001F) {
            if (psionDensityMap.remove(key) != null) markDirty();
        } else {
            psionDensityMap.put(key, density);
            markDirty();
        }
    }

    // --- 鬲疲ｳ輔ワ繝・す繝･謫堺ｽ・---
    public int getMagicHash(BlockPos pos) {
        long key = getLocalKey(pos);
        return magicHashMap.getOrDefault(key, 0);
    }

    public void setMagicHash(BlockPos pos, int hash) {
        long key = getLocalKey(pos);
        if (hash == 0) {
            if (magicHashMap.remove(key) != null) markDirty();
        } else {
            magicHashMap.put(key, hash);
            markDirty();
        }
    }

    // --- 繧ｨ繝ｳ繝医Ο繝斐・謫堺ｽ・---
    public float getEntropy(BlockPos pos) {
        long key = getLocalKey(pos);
        return entropyMap.getOrDefault(key, 0.0F);
    }

    public void setEntropy(BlockPos pos, float entropy) {
        long key = getLocalKey(pos);
        setEntropyDirect(key, entropy);
    }

    // 笆 霑ｽ蜉: 繝代こ繝・ヨ蜿嶺ｿ｡譎ら畑
    public void setEntropyDirect(long key, float entropy) {
        if (entropy <= 0.001F) {
            if (entropyMap.remove(key) != null) markDirty();
        } else {
            entropyMap.put(key, entropy);
            markDirty();
        }
    }

    private long getLocalKey(BlockPos pos) {
        // 荳倶ｽ・繝薙ャ繝・0-15)縺ｨY蠎ｧ讓吶ｒ菴ｿ縺｣縺ｦ繧ｭ繝ｼ逕滓・
        return BlockPos.asLong(pos.getX() & 0xF, pos.getY(), pos.getZ() & 0xF);
    }

    public void markDirty() {
        this.isDirty = true;
    }

    public boolean isDirty() {
        return isDirty;
    }

    public void setDirty(boolean dirty) {
        this.isDirty = dirty;
    }

    // --- NBT Serialization ---
    public CompoundTag serializeNBT() {
        CompoundTag tag = new CompoundTag();

        if (!temperatureMap.isEmpty()) {
            ListTag list = new ListTag();
            for (Map.Entry<Long, Float> entry : temperatureMap.entrySet()) {
                CompoundTag entryTag = new CompoundTag();
                entryTag.putLong("P", entry.getKey());
                entryTag.putFloat("V", entry.getValue());
                list.add(entryTag);
            }
            tag.put("TempMap", list);
        }

        if (!psionDensityMap.isEmpty()) {
            ListTag list = new ListTag();
            for (Map.Entry<Long, Float> entry : psionDensityMap.entrySet()) {
                CompoundTag entryTag = new CompoundTag();
                entryTag.putLong("P", entry.getKey());
                entryTag.putFloat("V", entry.getValue());
                list.add(entryTag);
            }
            tag.put("PsionMap", list);
        }

        if (!magicHashMap.isEmpty()) {
            ListTag list = new ListTag();
            for (Map.Entry<Long, Integer> entry : magicHashMap.entrySet()) {
                CompoundTag entryTag = new CompoundTag();
                entryTag.putLong("P", entry.getKey());
                entryTag.putInt("V", entry.getValue());
                list.add(entryTag);
            }
            tag.put("MagicMap", list);
        }

        if (!entropyMap.isEmpty()) {
            ListTag list = new ListTag();
            for (Map.Entry<Long, Float> entry : entropyMap.entrySet()) {
                CompoundTag entryTag = new CompoundTag();
                entryTag.putLong("P", entry.getKey());
                entryTag.putFloat("V", entry.getValue());
                list.add(entryTag);
            }
            tag.put("EntropyMap", list);
        }

        return tag;
    }

    public void deserializeNBT(CompoundTag tag) {
        temperatureMap.clear();
        if (tag.contains("TempMap")) {
            ListTag list = tag.getList("TempMap", Tag.TAG_COMPOUND);
            for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) {
                CompoundTag entry = list.getCompound(i);
                temperatureMap.put(entry.getLong("P"), entry.getFloat("V"));
            }
        }

        psionDensityMap.clear();
        if (tag.contains("PsionMap")) {
            ListTag list = tag.getList("PsionMap", Tag.TAG_COMPOUND);
            for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) {
                CompoundTag entry = list.getCompound(i);
                psionDensityMap.put(entry.getLong("P"), entry.getFloat("V"));
            }
        }

        magicHashMap.clear();
        if (tag.contains("MagicMap")) {
            ListTag list = tag.getList("MagicMap", Tag.TAG_COMPOUND);
            for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) {
                CompoundTag entry = list.getCompound(i);
                magicHashMap.put(entry.getLong("P"), entry.getInt("V"));
            }
        }

        entropyMap.clear();
        if (tag.contains("EntropyMap")) {
            ListTag list = tag.getList("EntropyMap", Tag.TAG_COMPOUND);
            for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) {
                CompoundTag entry = list.getCompound(i);
                entropyMap.put(entry.getLong("P"), entry.getFloat("V"));
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.world.cardinal;

import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.entity.EntitySciencePhenomenon;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.PhysicsMetadata;
import com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.VisualMetadata;
import com.mojang.math.Vector3f;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.ListTag;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ChunkPos;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.saveddata.SavedData;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

public class WorldCardinalSystem extends SavedData {

    private static final String DATA_NAME = "collabomod_cardinal_system";

    // 繝√Ε繝ｳ繧ｯ縺斐→縺ｮ迺ｰ蠅・ョ繝ｼ繧ｿ繧剃ｿ晄戟
    private final Map<Long, EnvironmentChunkData> chunkDataMap = new HashMap<>();
    private final ServerLevel level;

    // 譁ｰ隕丈ｽ懈・逕ｨ繧ｳ繝ｳ繧ｹ繝医Λ繧ｯ繧ｿ
    public WorldCardinalSystem(ServerLevel level) {
        this.level = level;
    }

    // 繝ｭ繝ｼ繝臥畑繧ｳ繝ｳ繧ｹ繝医Λ繧ｯ繧ｿ
    public WorldCardinalSystem(CompoundTag tag, ServerLevel level) {
        this.level = level;
        load(tag);
    }

    // 繧､繝ｳ繧ｹ繧ｿ繝ｳ繧ｹ蜿門ｾ・(Get or Create)
    public static WorldCardinalSystem get(ServerLevel level) {
        return level.getDataStorage().computeIfAbsent(
                tag -> new WorldCardinalSystem(tag, level),
                () -> new WorldCardinalSystem(level),
                DATA_NAME
        );
    }

    // 豈撒ick縺ｮ蜃ｦ逅・(CardinalTickHandler縺九ｉ蜻ｼ縺ｰ繧後ｋ)
    public void tick(ServerLevel level) {
        // 蜷・メ繝｣繝ｳ繧ｯ繝・・繧ｿ縺ｮ譖ｴ譁ｰ蜃ｦ逅・(EnvironmentChunkData#tick 繧貞他縺ｳ蜃ｺ縺・
        chunkDataMap.forEach((key, data) -> data.tick());

        // 螟画峩縺後≠繧後・菫晏ｭ倥ヵ繝ｩ繧ｰ繧堤ｫ九※繧・
        setDirty();
    }

    // 繝√Ε繝ｳ繧ｯ繝・・繧ｿ縺ｮ蜿門ｾ・
    public EnvironmentChunkData getChunkData(BlockPos pos) {
        long chunkKey = new ChunkPos(pos).toLong();
        return chunkDataMap.computeIfAbsent(chunkKey, k -> new EnvironmentChunkData());
    }

    /**
     * 迚ｩ逅・樟雎｡繧ｨ繝ｳ繝・ぅ繝・ぅ繧偵せ繝昴・繝ｳ縺輔○繧・
     * 菫ｮ豁｣: EntitySciencePhenomenon縺ｮ譛譁ｰ莉墓ｧ假ｼ医さ繝ｳ繧ｹ繝医Λ繧ｯ繧ｿ螟画峩・峨↓蟇ｾ蠢・
     */
    public void spawnPhenomenon(BlockPos pos, PhysicsMetadata physics, VisualMetadata visuals) {
        // 1. 繧ｨ繝ｳ繝・ぅ繝・ぅ逕滓・ (Environment襍ｷ蝗縺ｮ縺溘ａCaster縺ｯnull)
        EntitySciencePhenomenon phenomenon = new EntitySciencePhenomenon(level, null);

        // 2. 菴咲ｽｮ險ｭ螳・
        phenomenon.setPos(Vec3.atCenterOf(pos));

        // 3. 繝代Λ繝｡繝ｼ繧ｿ豕ｨ蜈･
        phenomenon.setPhysicsMetadata(physics);
        phenomenon.setVisualMetadata(visuals);

        // 4. 繝ｯ繝ｼ繝ｫ繝峨↓霑ｽ蜉
        level.addFreshEntity(phenomenon);
    }

    // 迺ｰ蠅・ｵｷ蝗縺ｮ邁｡譏鍋・逋ｺ縺ｪ縺ｩ繧定ｵｷ縺薙☆繝倥Ν繝代・
    public void createEnvironmentalBurst(BlockPos pos, float energy, float radius) {
        PhysicsMetadata physics = new PhysicsMetadata();
        physics.energy = energy;
        physics.forceType = PhysicsMetadata.EnumForceType.RADIAL;
        physics.velocity = 0.5f;
        physics.temperature = 1000.0f; // 鬮俶ｸｩ

        VisualMetadata visuals = new VisualMetadata();
        visuals.scale = radius;
        visuals.mainColor = new Vector3f(1.0f, 0.4f, 0.0f); // 繧ｪ繝ｬ繝ｳ繧ｸ
        visuals.shape = com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.EnumMagicShape.SPHERE;
        // VisualMetadata縺ｮ繝輔ぅ繝ｼ繝ｫ繝我ｻ墓ｧ伜､画峩縺ｫ莨ｴ縺・√い繝九Γ繝ｼ繧ｷ繝ｧ繝ｳ繧ｿ繧､繝礼ｭ峨・Timeline縺ｫ蜷ｫ縺ｾ繧後ｋ縺九・
        // 繧ゅ＠縺上・VisualMetadata縺ｮ莠呈鋤繝輔ぅ繝ｼ繝ｫ繝峨→縺励※險ｭ螳壹＠縺ｾ縺吶・
        // 縺薙％縺ｧ縺ｯVisualMetadata縺ｫanimationType繝輔ぅ繝ｼ繝ｫ繝峨′谿九▲縺ｦ縺・ｋ蜑肴署・亥燕蝗槭・菫ｮ豁｣貅匁侠・峨〒險倩ｿｰ縺励∪縺吶・
        visuals.animationType = com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.magic.EnumMagicAnimation.EXPAND_FADE;

        spawnPhenomenon(pos, physics, visuals);
    }

    public void addTemperature(Level level, BlockPos pos, float amount) {
        // 繧ｯ繝ｩ繧､繧｢繝ｳ繝亥・繧・∫ｮ｡逅・､悶・Level縺ｧ縺ゅｌ縺ｰ辟｡隕・
        if (level != this.level) return;

        EnvironmentChunkData data = getChunkData(pos);
        float current = data.getTemperature(pos);
        // 譁ｰ縺励＞貂ｩ蠎ｦ繧偵そ繝・ヨ (EnvironmentChunkData蜀・〒Dirty繝輔Λ繧ｰ遲峨・蜃ｦ逅・＆繧後ｋ)
        data.setTemperature(pos, current + amount);
    }

    // --- NBT Save/Load ---

    @Override
    public CompoundTag save(CompoundTag tag) {
        ListTag list = new ListTag();
        chunkDataMap.forEach((key, data) -> {
            CompoundTag chunkTag = new CompoundTag();
            chunkTag.putLong("ChunkKey", key);
            // EnvironmentChunkData#serializeNBT 繧貞他縺ｳ蜃ｺ縺・
            chunkTag.put("Data", data.serializeNBT());
            list.add(chunkTag);
        });
        tag.put("ChunkData", list);
        return tag;
    }

    public void load(CompoundTag tag) {
        if (tag.contains("ChunkData")) {
            ListTag list = tag.getList("ChunkData", 10);
            for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) {
                CompoundTag chunkTag = list.getCompound(i);
                long key = chunkTag.getLong("ChunkKey");

                EnvironmentChunkData data = new EnvironmentChunkData();
                // EnvironmentChunkData#deserializeNBT 繧貞他縺ｳ蜃ｺ縺・
                data.deserializeNBT(chunkTag.getCompound("Data"));

                chunkDataMap.put(key, data);
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.world.idea;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;

public class EidosData {
    // 蠎ｧ讓呎ュ蝣ｱ
    private final BlockPos position;
    // 迥ｶ諷具ｼ医ヶ繝ｭ繝・け縺ｪ繧隠lockState縲√お繝ｳ繝・ぅ繝・ぅ縺ｪ繧丑BT・・
    private final BlockState blockState;
    private final CompoundTag entityData;
    // 險倬鹸縺輔ｌ縺滓凾蛻ｻ・医Ρ繝ｼ繝ｫ繝画凾髢難ｼ・
    private final long timestamp;

    // 繝悶Ο繝・け逕ｨ繧ｳ繝ｳ繧ｹ繝医Λ繧ｯ繧ｿ
    public EidosData(BlockPos pos, BlockState state, long time) {
        this.position = pos;
        this.blockState = state;
        this.entityData = null;
        this.timestamp = time;
    }

    // 繧ｨ繝ｳ繝・ぅ繝・ぅ逕ｨ繧ｳ繝ｳ繧ｹ繝医Λ繧ｯ繧ｿ
    public EidosData(LivingEntity entity, long time) {
        this.position = entity.blockPosition();
        this.blockState = null;
        this.entityData = new CompoundTag();
        entity.saveWithoutId(this.entityData); // 迴ｾ蝨ｨ縺ｮ繧ｨ繝ｳ繝・ぅ繝・ぅ諠・ｱ繧誰BT縺ｨ縺励※菫晏ｭ・
        this.timestamp = time;
    }

    public BlockPos getPosition() { return position; }
    public BlockState getBlockState() { return blockState; }
    public CompoundTag getEntityData() { return entityData; }
    public long getTimestamp() { return timestamp; }
}

package com.COLLABOMOD.collabomod.world.idea;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.saveddata.SavedData;

import java.util.*;

public class IdeaDimensionData extends SavedData {

    // 繧ｷ繝ｳ繧ｰ繝ｫ繝医Φ逧・↓繧｢繧ｯ繧ｻ繧ｹ縺吶ｋ縺溘ａ縺ｮID
    private static final String DATA_NAME = "collabo_mod_idea_dimension";

    // 笆 繧ｨ繧､繝峨せ縺ｮ螻･豁ｴ菫晉ｮ｡蠎ｫ (繧､繝・い)
    // UUID (繧ｨ繝ｳ繝・ぅ繝・ぅ) -> 螻･豁ｴ繝ｪ繧ｹ繝・
    private final Map<UUID, LinkedList<EidosData>> entityHistory = new HashMap<>();

    // BlockPos (繝悶Ο繝・け) -> 螻･豁ｴ繝ｪ繧ｹ繝・
    // 窶ｻ譛ｬ譚･縺ｯ蜈ｨ繝悶Ο繝・け繧定ｨ倬鹸縺吶∋縺阪〒縺吶′縲∬ｲ闕ｷ蟇ｾ遲悶〒縲檎ｴ螢翫＆繧後◆蝣ｴ謇縲阪・縺ｿ荳譎りｨ倬鹸縺励∪縺・
    private final Map<BlockPos, EidosData> blockHistory = new HashMap<>();

    // 螻･豁ｴ縺ｮ菫晄戟謨ｰ荳企剞・亥・謌舌〒縺阪ｋ髯千阜・・
    private static final int MAX_HISTORY = 20;

    // 繝・・繧ｿ縺ｮ蜿門ｾ暦ｼ医↑縺代ｌ縺ｰ譁ｰ隕丈ｽ懈・・・
    public static IdeaDimensionData get(ServerLevel level) {
        return level.getDataStorage().computeIfAbsent(
                IdeaDimensionData::load,
                IdeaDimensionData::create,
                DATA_NAME
        );
    }

    public static IdeaDimensionData create() {
        return new IdeaDimensionData();
    }

    public static IdeaDimensionData load(CompoundTag nbt) {
        // 繧ｵ繝ｼ繝舌・蜀崎ｵｷ蜍墓凾縺ｫ繝・・繧ｿ繧貞ｾｩ蜈・☆繧句・逅・ｼ井ｻ雁屓縺ｯ隍・尅縺ｫ縺ｪ繧九・縺ｧ逵∫払縲√Γ繝｢繝ｪ蜀・・縺ｿ縺ｧ邂｡逅・ｼ・
        return new IdeaDimensionData();
    }

    @Override
    public CompoundTag save(CompoundTag nbt) {
        // 繧ｻ繝ｼ繝門・逅・ｼ井ｻ雁屓縺ｯ逵∫払・・
        return nbt;
    }

    // 笆笆笆 莠玖ｱ｡縺ｮ險倬鹸・医お繧､繝峨せ縺ｮ菫晏ｭ假ｼ・笆笆笆

    // 繧ｨ繝ｳ繝・ぅ繝・ぅ縺後ム繝｡繝ｼ繧ｸ繧貞女縺代◆譎ゅ↑縺ｩ縺ｫ蜻ｼ縺ｶ
    public void recordEntityState(LivingEntity entity, long time) {
        UUID id = entity.getUUID();
        entityHistory.putIfAbsent(id, new LinkedList<>());

        LinkedList<EidosData> history = entityHistory.get(id);
        // 迴ｾ蝨ｨ縺ｮ迥ｶ諷九ｒ菫晏ｭ・
        history.addFirst(new EidosData(entity, time));

        // 蜿､縺・ョ繝ｼ繧ｿ縺ｯ豸医☆・医う繝・い縺ｮ螳ｹ驥冗ｯ邏・ｼ・
        if (history.size() > MAX_HISTORY) {
            history.removeLast();
        }
        this.setDirty(); // 菫晏ｭ倥ヵ繝ｩ繧ｰ
    }

    // 笆笆笆 莠玖ｱ｡縺ｮ隱ｭ縺ｿ蜃ｺ縺暦ｼ亥・謌千畑・・笆笆笆

    // 謖・ｮ壹＠縺溘お繝ｳ繝・ぅ繝・ぅ縺ｮ縲・縺､蜑阪阪・繧ｨ繧､繝峨せ繧貞叙繧雁・縺・
    /*
    public EidosData getPreviousEntityState(UUID uuid) {
        if (entityHistory.containsKey(uuid)) {
            LinkedList<EidosData> history = entityHistory.get(uuid);
            if (!history.isEmpty()) {
                // 譛譁ｰ縺ｮ螻･豁ｴ・育峩蜑阪・辟｡蛯ｷ縺ｮ迥ｶ諷具ｼ峨ｒ霑斐☆
                return history.getFirst();
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

     */
    // 笆 霑ｽ蜉: 譛驕ｩ縺ｪ・域怙繧・P縺碁ｫ倥＞・峨お繧､繝峨せ繧呈､懃ｴ｢縺励※蜿門ｾ・
    public EidosData getOptimalEntityState(UUID uuid) {
        if (entityHistory.containsKey(uuid)) {
            LinkedList<EidosData> history = entityHistory.get(uuid);
            if (history.isEmpty()) return null;

            // 螻･豁ｴ縺ｮ荳ｭ縺ｧ譛繧・P縺碁ｫ倥＞繝・・繧ｿ繧呈爾縺・
            EidosData bestState = null;
            float maxHealthFound = -1.0F;

            for (EidosData data : history) {
                CompoundTag tag = data.getEntityData();
                if (tag.contains("Health")) {
                    float hp = tag.getFloat("Health");
                    if (hp > maxHealthFound) {
                        maxHealthFound = hp;
                        bestState = data;
                    }
                }
            }
            // 隕九▽縺九ｌ縺ｰ縺昴ｌ繧偵√↑縺代ｌ縺ｰ譛譁ｰ・育峩蜑搾ｼ峨ｒ霑斐☆
            return bestState != null ? bestState : history.getFirst();
        }
        return null;
    }

    // 螻･豁ｴ繧貞炎髯､縺吶ｋ繝｡繧ｽ繝・ラ
    public void clearHistory(UUID uuid) {
        if (entityHistory.containsKey(uuid)) {
            entityHistory.remove(uuid);
            this.setDirty();
        }
    }
}
